# Agent log

Newest entry on top. One entry per run: date, chunk, what changed, commit,
what is next, defaults applied.

Older entries are in [`log/`](log/): [1–19 November 2026](log/2026-11-01-to-19.md) and [October 2026](log/2026-10.md).

## 2026-11-25 · chat · availability: each day's suggested hours from the business's own shifts

- On chat/fill-suggest from origin/main 9c48b8a (#257). The plan's PR 3b, from the review of part 1: workers who kept the availability form's suggested hours were rarely placed by the fill, because those hours (09:00–17:00, then 18:00–23:00; Friday 08:00 until 15:00 or candle lighting; Saturday havdalah to 23:30) didn't cover whole shifts. No migration, no new package, no change in `packages/ui`. New words, from the review: a line in the day's sheet naming the day's shifts (`app.availability.shiftHours`) and a sentence more in the regular shifts' hint (`modules.scheduling.settings.shifts.hint`), both in both languages; the form's other words, its three shapes, checks and deadline are as they were. A visible change: every business with shifts sees its own hours in an empty day, and the day's sheet names each shift's hours.
- `@domain/availability-form`: `suggestedDay(day, shabbat, shifts)` is what an empty day opens with: from the earliest start to the latest end of the business's shifts that day (an end past midnight on the next day, as the ranges allow), each shift first cut where the form's Shabbat rules cut the day (Friday ends by candle lighting, Saturday starts from havdalah) and one wholly inside Shabbat left out. Hours kept as suggested so cover every shift that day that Shabbat leaves whole; a shift Shabbat cuts stays open in the fill with its time named, as before. Shifts around the clock (a day or more from the first start to the last end) give two ranges that meet at the last shift's start, since a range is shorter than a day; joined, they cover it. A day with no shift, or none Shabbat leaves, opens with the usual day as before (`usualRange`, today's code). `suggestedRange(day, rows, shabbat, shifts)` ("הוספת שעות") gives an empty day its first range, and a day with hours something that overlaps none of its rows whenever anything can, in this order: what the day's shifts still need (the part of the day's suggested hours the rows leave free, after them if any is, never one starting past midnight), the usual evening, the day's latest free hours until midnight inside Shabbat's limits and no longer than the usual ones (the restaurant's 09:00–23:30 gets 23:30–00:00), and only on a day with no free minute the usual hours all the same. `shiftHoursOn(day, shabbat, shifts)` is what the day's sheet lists: each of the day's shifts once, earliest first, in its own hours, one wholly inside Shabbat left out. `ShiftHours` (a pattern's days and hours, never its name or how many it needs) and `shiftHoursOf`. `@domain/staffing`'s `shiftsOn` is generic, so the form reads a day's shifts as the builder draws them.
- The source: `AvailabilityWeek.shiftHours`, from `weekContext`'s `settings.shifts` (the scheduling manifest's `shifts`, which the template fills and `/manage/scheduling` edits), the same patterns the builder draws a week from (`context.patterns`). `openDraft` takes the week's Shabbat and shift hours; the day sheet's "הוספת שעות" passes them. The twins: the availability twin's week has the restaurant template's hours (`demoShiftPatterns`), the shop's running-week twin the shop's.
- Tests: `availability-form.test.ts`: `shiftHoursOf`; `suggestedDay` (the templates' days, a break between shifts inside, only that weekday's shifts; Friday cut at candle lighting and Saturday at havdalah, each shift before spanning, summer's havdalah after 19:30 and Jerusalem's December candles before the café's 16:00; open on Shabbat as any day; the usual day with no shifts, with none on Saturday, and with every shift inside Shabbat; around the clock, more than a day, and a minute short of one); 63 sets of shifts (the three templates and 60 generated), each closed on Shabbat three ways and open, every day: hours the form takes, at most two ranges, covering each shift whole exactly when Shabbat doesn't cut it, and the second range valid on its own; `suggestedRange` (the old cases with no shifts, an empty day's first range, each step of its order with its own cases, the sheet's own steps for each template every day, and in the 63 sets the added hours beside the day's as it opens wherever there is room, `roomBeside`); `shiftHoursOn` (each shift once, earliest first, in its own hours where Shabbat cuts what can be marked, one wholly inside Shabbat left out). `fill-week.test.ts`: the test that showed the gap now shows, per template (restaurant, café, shop) with and without Shabbat, and for day-and-night shifts, every shift covered by every kept answer, which the form would send as it is; ten people fill every place (44, 37, 23, 14), and three leave places open only where everyone off the shift covers it and a rule stopped them. A new one: kept answers leave open only what Shabbat cuts (the restaurant's and the café's Saturday night in summer, `shabbat` with havdalah; the café's Friday in Jerusalem's December, with candle lighting), everything else filled. For the PR body: five people keeping the suggestion now fill the restaurant's 29 of 44 places (20 before), the café's 25 of 37 (0) and the shop's 23 of 23 (3), closed on Shabbat in December or open, every place left open `blocked` by a rule. `e2e/availability.spec.ts`: Sunday opens 09:00–23:30, Friday 08:00–14:30, Saturday at the later of 19:30 and havdalah until 00:00, and what's sent keeps those ends.
- Docs: `packages/domain/README.md` (a paragraph on `availability-form.ts`), ROADMAP's scheduling row.
- Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright (`availability.spec.ts` changed; `builder-focus.spec.ts`'s availability twin now adds 17:00–23:30 after Sunday's 09:00–17:00, which it doesn't name), pgTAP, screenshots at 390, 820 and 1440 (the form's layout is unchanged but for the day sheet's subtitle, which gains a line on days with shifts); CI runs them.
- Changed from the plan: `suggestedRange` is in `availability-form.ts`, not `availability.ts`; the form has no rule for holidays (only Shabbat's), so the suggestion has none; the Friday 15:00 cap was today's default, not a Shabbat rule, so with a Friday shift only candle lighting cuts (the café's 07:00–16:00 is covered in Tel Aviv's December), and 15:00 stays for a Friday without shifts; each shift is cut by Shabbat before the day is spanned, so a Saturday morning inside Shabbat doesn't pull the day back to havdalah; shifts around the clock can't be one range, so they are two; the old test's answers with the second range kept aren't sendable now (it overlaps the first on most days), so the test keeps each day as it opens. The plan's §4.2 "café and bar, and retail: as soon as anyone sends availability" holds now for answers kept as suggested.
- Next: PR 2 (one way back for anything that changes only the builder's screen), then PR 3 and PR 4.
- Defaults applied, to ask Orel: (1) an empty day opens with all of the day's shifts, from the first's start to the last's end (09:00–23:30 at the restaurant on a weekday), so whoever keeps it may be put on any of them; the alternatives were the fill taking near-fits, or the day opening with its first shift only; (2) "הוספת שעות" on a day with hours adds what the day's shifts still need, otherwise other free hours that day, and an overlap only where nothing else fits, rather than always 18:00–23:00; (3) with a Friday shift, only candle lighting cuts it, not 15:00; (4) shifts around the clock open as two ranges; (5) the suggestion comes from the shift pattern, not from a week the manager already saved (a worker reads no draft); (6) under the day's title the sheet names the hours of each of the day's shifts, each in its own hours ("המשמרות ביום הזה: …", Question 1's option א, taken after review), rather than no words saying where the day's hours come from.
- After review (nine findings, each confirmed; three of them the same bug, two of them questions for Orel): (1, 3, 8) "הוספת שעות" never looked at the day's hours, so on every Sunday to Thursday with shifts it added hours inside the ones the day opens with (the restaurant's 09:00–23:30 got 16:00–23:30, the café's 07:00–00:00 got 15:00–00:00, the shop's 08:00–22:00 got 15:00–22:00, a day of one shift 10:00–20:00 the usual 18:00–23:00, shifts around the clock their second range again), and "סיום" then refused hours the worker never typed; an answer kept from before (09:00–17:00) got an hour's overlap where main gave 18:00–23:00. `suggestedRange(day, rows, shabbat, shifts)` now takes the day's rows (the sheet passes them) and adds hours that overlap none of them whenever any can, in this order: an empty day's first suggested range, as before; what the day's shifts still need, the part of the day's suggested hours the rows leave free, after them if any is (09:00–16:00 or 09:00–17:00 at the restaurant gets the evening until 23:30, which joined covers the evening shift; the café's kept 09:00–17:00 gets 17:00–00:00, not its 07:00–09:00), otherwise the earliest (16:00–23:30 gets 09:00–16:00), never a part starting past midnight, which would read as the morning; the usual evening, where it is free; the day's latest free hours until midnight inside Shabbat's limits, beside the day's hours and no longer than the usual ones (23:30–00:00 at the restaurant on a weekday, 14:30 until candle lighting on its Friday, 17:24–19:30 on its Saturday, the five hours before 19:30 when open on Shabbat). A row with a time cleared isn't counted. Only a day with no free minute the form takes (the restaurant's and the café's Saturday in summer, from havdalah to midnight and past it) still gets the usual hours, and the day's check names the overlap, as on main. Friday and Saturday, which doubled before this change too, now don't. Tests: the sheet's own steps for each template, closed on Shabbat three ways and open, every day (the day as it opens, then "הוספת שעות", passing `dayProblems` together; the two summer Saturdays give the overlap), the usual 09:00–17:00 kept Sunday to Thursday (passing together, and covering every shift from 09:00 on, joined); in the 63 sets of shifts, the added hours beside the day's as it opens, where a day they fail on must have no room at all (`roomBeside`, which tries with `dayProblems` the minutes where hours could first be free: midnight, havdalah and each row's end; 72 of the 1,764 days, mostly a Saturday from havdalah past midnight); each step's own cases. Each step's mutation (no midnight rule, no "after" preference, no length limit either side, rows not whole counted, the earliest free hours instead of the latest, no usual evening, rows ignored, Shabbat's window either day, `shiftHoursOn` listing a shift twice) fails the tests, checked by mutating the file. `builder-focus.spec.ts`'s Sunday (09:00–17:00) now adds 17:00–23:30, which it doesn't name. (2, 6) Question 1 taken on its recommendation (option א), as Orel asks for open questions: under the day's title, after the Shabbat line, the sheet lists each of the day's shifts' hours, each once and in its own hours (first cut by Shabbat as the suggestion is, which the check below undid), only on a day with shifts (`shiftHoursOn`): "המשמרות ביום הזה: 09:00–16:00 ו-16:00–23:30. אם אתם יכולים לעבוד רק בחלק מהן, סמנו רק את השעות שלהן." and on a day of one shift only its first sentence (`app.availability.shiftHours`, with its English twin; the hours in isolates, in the locale's list). This replaces default (6); undone by dropping that line from `dayHint`. So the PR has new words after all. (4) The regular shifts' hint on `/manage/scheduling` and the console now says each day of the availability form opens with their hours, and that answers already sent stay until the worker sends again, as closed-on-Shabbat's hint says (`modules.scheduling.settings.shifts.hint`, both languages). (5) ROADMAP's row: the Shabbat cut only for a business closed on Shabbat, a shift Shabbat cuts stays open, the usual hours as before on a day without shifts, and the new line and "הוספת שעות". (7) Question 2 stays open for Orel with its recommended option already what the code does (default (5): the suggestion reads the shift pattern, never a week the manager saved), so nothing changed. (9) Not changed: the remove button's tooltip reads the hours backwards ("הסרת השעות {from}–{to}" through plain `t()`, from #23); it is older than this branch, which doesn't touch it, so it is its own change (the review's fix: `<ltr>` in both messages, `t.markup` with `isolate`, and `builder-focus.spec.ts`'s name for the button). Default (2) now reads: "הוספת שעות" adds what the day's shifts still need, otherwise other free hours that day, and an overlap only where nothing else fits. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here, as before: `pnpm build`, Playwright, pgTAP, screenshots at 390, 820 and 1440 (the day sheet's subtitle gains a line on days with shifts).
- After the check (two findings, both confirmed): (1) the day's sheet named Shabbat-cut hours as the shift's own, under "המשמרת ביום הזה": the restaurant's summer Saturday read 20:21–00:00 for its 19:30–00:00, the café's 20:21–01:00 for 19:00–01:00, the café's Friday in Jerusalem's December 07:00–15:58 for 07:00–16:00, and a winter Saturday shift 09:00–18:00 became a 36-minute 17:24–18:00 that doesn't exist. `shiftHoursOn` now lists each shift's own hours, each once, earliest first, and leaves out only one wholly inside Shabbat (`cutByShabbat`, now shared with `shiftSpans`); the Shabbat line above it already says what can be marked, and the cut stays only in `suggestedDay` and `suggestedRange`, so the day still opens with 20:21–00:00.
