## 2026-11-25 · chat · reduced motion turns transitions off

- `app/globals.css`: under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`, every element's transitions are now `0s` (and no delay) instead of `0.01ms`. A transition of 0.01ms is still one of every property, `visibility` included, so an element shown by the same drawing that put the focus on it stayed hidden for a frame or two and refused that focus. #253 found it in the hiring file shown again after its check (in CI, where Playwright asks for reduced motion) and works around it there; this removes the cause for everyone who asks for less motion. Animations keep their `0.01ms` and one iteration, since Radix's sheets wait for `animationend`; nothing in the app or its packages waits for a `transitionend`.
- Tests: `tooling/reduced-motion.test.ts` (the block's selector, transitions at 0s with no delay, animations still ending). Checked here: `pnpm check` (two repo-wide tooling tests timed out at 5s under load and pass on their own). Not run here: Playwright, which runs every spec with reduced motion, so CI shows whether any spec leaned on the old 0.01ms.
- Defaults applied, to ask Orel: none.
## 2026-11-25 · chat · filling the schedule from availability, part 1: the rule

- On chat/fill-rule from origin/main 257809c (#254). The first of the plan's PRs for "מילוי אוטומטי מהזמינות" (ROADMAP "אחר כך", approved 22.11 as part of daily-use readiness): `@domain` only. No screen, no words, no migration, no new package; ROADMAP and `docs/AGENT.md` stay as they are until the builder's pill (PR 4).
- `@domain/fill-week`: `fillFromAvailability` fills a week's open places (shifts still to start that need more people) from the staff's answers, only appending people, never moving, removing or re-positioning anyone or changing a shift. Only staff who sent an answer whose ranges cover the whole shift are placed (owners only when they sent one), and only while every rule holds, in this order, the first that fails being the person's reason: `away` (`awayDuring`), `minorHours` (08:00–20:00, `FILL_MINOR_HOURS`, from `PAYROLL_READINGS.minorNight.school`), `minorLong` (8 hours, 7 on a Friday), `busy`, `sameDay`, `rest` (8 hours, the weeks either side included, in real time since the second review), `days` (`FILL_MAX_DAYS`, 6) and `hours` (`weekHours`, 42 by default as `FILL_WEEK_HOURS`, up to `FILL_WEEK_HOURS_MAX`, 58; for a minor 40 when that is less, `fillWeekCap` since the second review). The scarcest place goes first; the fewest hours this week, then the fewest of that kind of shift, the fewest shifts, the fewest hours last week, then an FNV-1a rotation of the week and the id. Positions from last week. Each place left open says why: `shabbat` (with candle lighting or havdalah), `nobody` (with how many cover only part), or `blocked` (everyone who covers it, with their first rule). Rule-per-pair cache, worked out again only for the person just placed. `weekMinors` (who is a minor on any day of the week, from `getTeamYouth`'s map, ids only) is for PR 3's `currentBuildMinors`.
- `@domain/schedule`: the private `span` is the exported `shiftSpan`, on `daysApart`, so a day of another week has its own place (last Saturday day -1, next Sunday day 7); the same inside the week. `@domain/copy-week` drops its `spanOf` and `(index - 7) * 1440` for it, keeping its guard that a shift outside last week is never read as an edge (pinned by a test). `@domain/absences`: `awayDuring` (the shift's day, and the next for a night past midnight; one ending at 00:00 is its own day's), not used by the screens yet (PR 3). The package README has a section.
- Tests: `fill-week.test.ts`: what it keeps, who it places, each rule on its own (a night into a day away and a shift ending at 00:00, the minor's hours, length, Friday and 40 hours at 58, `busy`, `sameDay` at 8 hours apart, the café's evening to 07:00, last week's Saturday night only when it is the week just before, next Sunday's 07:00 against Saturday's night, a seventh day, the manager's hours counted), each tie-break on its own, the rotation changing by week and never by order, a team free alike within one shift, evenings shared, the scarcest place first, positions (same kind, else most, ties by the business's order, a removed one null), the reasons, a Monday week, hand shifts with no pattern, the café's and the shop's patterns, 12-hour days and nights, the restaurant's 44 places, shuffled input giving the same fill, 300 generated weeks (every rule and reason reached; the result always passes `weekSchedule`, adds no `doubleBooked` pair, and whoever it adds is covered, not away, `available` in the shift's sheet, within the minors', rest, day and hour limits), 200 shifts by 50 people (well under a second here), and the three tables the PR shows (the restaurant twin, the café's rest case, the shop's Tamar as a minor). `schedule.test.ts` (`shiftSpan` equal to the old span in the week; days -1, 7, 8), `absences.test.ts` (`awayDuring`), `copy-week.test.ts` (the guard).
- Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright, pgTAP (nothing they cover changed); CI runs them.
- Changed from the plan: the restaurant twin's Noa is already on Sunday morning, so the fill adds Shiran there (and on Thursday evening) and Noa on Saturday night: 3 places, 12 left open; a position is matched by the kind of shift (its pattern, or its name and start when added by hand) rather than the pattern key alone; next week's shifts dated inside the week are never edges; `nobody` means nobody else, so the sheet (PR 4) should say "אף אחד נוסף" whenever anyone is on the shift after the fill, whoever put them there (after the second review, fix 3); the copy's "night into a day away" test goes with PR 3, which switches the copy to `awayDuring`.
- Next: PR 2, one way back for anything that changes only the builder's screen.
- Defaults applied, to ask Orel (the plan's §8.2): only availability that covers the whole shift (4); owners only when they sent availability (5); it only adds (6); one shift a day (7); at most 6 days (8); 8 hours between shifts, the weeks either side included (9); 42 hours a week, minors 40 (10, the setting is PR 5); the 36-hour weekly rest not checked (11); a day away counts for the night into it (12); the fairness order (13); the scarcest place first (14); positions from last week (15); a shift into Shabbat stays open with its time named (16).
- After review (ten findings, each confirmed): (1) `shabbat` was the reason whenever a shift reached into Shabbat, even beside someone the fill put on it or someone a rule stopped, so its words ("ולכן אין לה זמינות") would have been false and the person's own rule hidden. It is the reason now only while no answer covers the shift, of anyone on it (whether the fill or, since the check, the manager put them there) or of anyone else; otherwise the reason is `nobody` or `blocked`, with `FillShift.shabbat` still set so the sheet (PR 4) can add the Shabbat line beside it (`FillWhy` and `shabbat` documented so, and the README). This turns the plan's §2.6 order around for those two cases, and with it the review's own proposal that Shabbat come before the people it stopped: a Saturday night covered by an answer sent before the business closed, with Noa away, gives `blocked` (Noa, `away`) with Shabbat beside it, and the same shift needing two gives Noa and then `nobody`. (2) Workers who keep the form's suggested hours (`suggestedRange`: 09:00–17:00, then 18:00–23:00; Friday 08:00 to 15:00 or candle lighting; Saturday havdalah to 23:30) are placed only where those hours cover a shift whole. With five such people in a December week, the café fills 0 of 37 places, the shop 3 of 23 (Friday, only while closed on Shabbat), the restaurant 20 of 44 (15 when open on Shabbat). The rule works as the plan says, but the plan's §4.2 "café and bar, and retail: as soon as anyone sends availability" isn't true for such answers, and the PR's body should say so. A new test pins which of each template's shifts those hours fill. A question for Orel before PR 4: should the form suggest each day's hours from the business's own shifts (template data, so no setup), or should the fill take near-fits? No rule leaves the restaurant twin's 12 places open (nobody is blocked): 5 are on shifts that others cover only in part, and 7 are on shifts no other answer reaches. (3–10) Each mutation the review found surviving is now killed (checked by mutating the file and running the tests): the fairness keys each set against the next, with the rotation's winner this week (Yoav) as the baseline, the two old fixtures rewritten (fewest hours before fewest shifts, the kind before the shifts, the shifts before the rotation); a shift that has begun still counts (no second shift that day); 42 hours, and a minor's 40, reached exactly, placed; the rules' order (away, the minor's hours, the minor's length, busy; the same day before rest; rest, days, hours); someone already on an open shift neither blocked nor partial, and someone stopped not partial; a shift dated outside last week never read as last week's, with the two-weeks-back case now saying what holds it (`shiftSpan`; that only the week just before counts at all is held by last week's hours among the tie-breaks); a minor's shift ending at 20:00 exactly; two shifts added by hand with one name told apart by their start. `fill-week.test.ts` has 58 tests. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`.
- After the check (three findings): (1) the Shabbat reason still hung on who placed the person whose answer covers the shift: a Saturday night into Shabbat with Noa's answer covering it gave `nobody` when the fill put her on and `shabbat` when the manager had, though both weeks end the same, and in the second the words "ולכן אין לה זמינות" were false again. `why` now looks at the answer of everyone on the shift after the fill, whoever put them there, so both give `nobody` with Shabbat beside it; someone on it whose answer doesn't cover it, or who sent none, still leaves `shabbat` (the second way the finding offered, the one that keeps fix (1)'s words true; the first, `shabbat` whenever nobody off the shift covers it, would have brought back the false words for the fill's own placement). A new test pins both placements and the two that stay `shabbat`, and fails under either old condition (the fill's placements only, or anyone on it at all); `FillWhy`, the README and fix (1) above say so. (2) The Tests bullet's count (47, from before the review) is gone; `fill-week.test.ts` has 59 tests now. (3) The restaurant twin's test now pins every row of the PR's table (the day, start, who the fill adds, what stays open, why and how many cover part), with no positions, nobody blocked and nothing into Shabbat, in place of its four rows; nothing else to fix there. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`.
- After the second review (four findings, each confirmed in the code): (1) `rest` was measured on the wall clock, so the nights the clocks change gave the wrong answer. In the week of 22.3.2026 (and of 21.3.2027) the restaurant template's Thursday evening (16:00–23:30) and Friday morning (08:00–14:30) are 7½ real hours apart, and the fill put one person on both; a night until 01:30, then 09:30, is 7; in the week of 25.10.2026, last week's Saturday until 00:30, then Sunday's 07:30, is 8 and was refused. `rest` now compares real instants, each shift placed as `private.shift_span` places it (`instantIn` on its day, the end on the next day past midnight), as payroll's `restBetweenUnder8` measures; `busy`, `sameDay` and the week's hours stay on the wall clock (the hours as the builder's totals count them, so the cap agrees with what the screen shows). Last week's shifts are kept as edges by the same test in real time (ending less than 8 real hours before the week begins), which in Israel's zone keeps the same ones as before. Each date and time is read once a fill, and `instantIn`'s formatter is now made once per zone (as `dateIn`'s is), so the 300 generated weeks run as fast as before (0.8 s here). Tests: the three weeks above, with the week of 29.3.2026 as the control (8½ hours, placed); a check that those are the nights the clocks change; and the generated weeks now include 22.3.2026, 25.10.2026 and the Monday week of 19.10.2026, with the invariant's rest measured in real time (it fails as well when the wall clock is put back). (2) `fillWeekCap(weekHours, minor)`, in hours, is the one cap: the week's hours, or a minor's 40 when that is less. The fill and the test's invariant use it, and PR 4's sheet names it for `hours` rather than 40: a minor with the setting at 30 is stopped at 30. `FillRule`'s doc and the README say so; the minors line's `{week}` 40 stays, as the ceiling. New tests: `fillWeekCap`'s values, and a minor stopped at 30 hours with the week at 30, placed at 30 exactly. For the plan (outside this repo): §1.7's note becomes "the `hours` reason for a minor names `fillWeekCap(weekHours, true)`", and PR 5's hint "מתחת לגיל 18 המילוי עוצר ב-40" becomes "מתחת לגיל 18 המילוי עוצר ב-40, או במספר שכאן אם הוא קטן יותר". (3) The words for `nobody` and Shabbat, for PR 4: when anyone is on the shift after the fill (`fill.week[s.index].assignees.length > 0`), whoever put them there, the sheet says `why.nobodyElse`, "אף אחד נוסף לא שלח זמינות לכל שעות המשמרת.", not only when the fill added someone (the "Changed from the plan" bullet above now says so). When `s.shabbat !== null && s.why !== "shabbat"`, an answer covers the shift, so the Shabbat line names the time without "ולכן אין לה זמינות": `why.fridayToo`, "המשמרת נגמרת אחרי כניסת השבת (<ltr>{time}</ltr>).", and `why.saturdayToo`, "המשמרת מתחילה לפני צאת השבת (<ltr>{time}</ltr>).". The plan's §2.6 should follow the code's order: `shabbat` only while no answer covers the shift (of anyone on it or anyone else), then `blocked` when someone who covers it was stopped, else `nobody`; §1.7 gains the three keys. `FillShift.why`, `FillShift.shabbat` and the README now say which words the sheet picks. No new test: the code already gives these cases (pinned by "gives the same reason whether the fill or the manager put on the one whose answer covers it" and "names whoever covers a shift into Shabbat and was stopped"); only the guidance was wrong. (4) `weekMinors` takes `Iterable<readonly [userId, youthUntil]>`, so `getTeamYouth`'s `Map` passes as it is; its test passes pairs, and a new one passes a `Map`. `fill-week.test.ts` has 66 tests. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`.

## 2026-11-24 · chat · accessibility, part 8: management and lists

- On chat/a11y-lists from origin/main 1837c80 (#250), with origin/main b7ef204 (#251) merged in after the check. The sweep's lists-me-file findings 39, 41, 42, 43, 44 and 54; 40, 45, 46, 47, 50 and 51 came with #243, and 48, 49, 52 and 53 are the sibling part. No migration, no new package, no SQL; in `packages/ui` ChecklistItem's `itemId` (`data-list-item`, on `/design`) and, after the second review, UndoRow's name (`namedByWords`, on `/design` with the gallery's rows); the server actions answer as before. New messages: `app.lists.list.reopened`, "הרשימה נפתחה שוב" / "The list is open again", and after the second review `ui.undoRow.named`, "ביטול: {message}" / "Undo: {message}".
- F39, who sees pay (`/manage/access`, and the console's): a person's "הסרה" goes with their row, so the undo's "ביטול" takes the focus as it appears (UndoRow `autoFocus`, a row of its own for each person). Its six seconds and the removal it holds are each a `heldTimer` now, which stops while "ביטול" is being reached (`onWaitingChange` to `personWaits`: the slot's `waits` and the held removal's `pause`/`resume`, each only for the person whose row it is, so a row going as the next person is removed lets go of nothing newer). Undone, the focus goes back to the person's "הסרה" (`data-access-remove`, `accessRemove`), brought into view; its time up, to the "גישה אישית" title. A role's switch keeps its focus: its "ביטול" waits the same way when reached, and gives the focus back to the switch, else "שעות ושכר". The slot is `createUndoSlot` in `manage/access/access-undo.ts`. Chosen over the roles screen's toast: the undo stays where it was, under what it undoes, as invites and departments do, and nothing on screen moves.
- F41, removing from a sheet (the shifts on `/manage/scheduling`, a list's items and its times in the template editor, and the items of a list typed when sending): the sheet closes onto the removal's "ביטול" (`useSheetReturn` in `manage/use-sheet-return.ts`: Sheet `returnFocus`, UndoRow `buttonRef` and `quiet`), so the removal is said by that focus once the sheet has gone, never while it hid the page; anything else closes onto what opened it, as before, and the section's title when that is gone (`sheetReturnTo` in `manage/editor-focus.ts`, PageSection `headingRef`). Undone, the focus is on the row that came back, brought into view: the item's (`data-item-row`), the shift's or the time's by its place in the group (`groupRow`).
- F42: a position's trash button goes with its row, so its "ביטול" takes the focus (`autoFocus`), and undone gives it to that position's trash button again (`data-position-remove`), not its name, a field a phone would open its keyboard for. A list photo's "ביטול" takes the focus the remove button kept while removing, unless the person put it elsewhere meanwhile; undone, the photo's remove button has it, and refused for good, "הוספת תמונה"; a way back that didn't get there gives its "ביטול" the focus again.
- F43: Lists right after sending, and the templates right after removing one in its editor: the pressed button went with the screen, so "ביטול" takes the focus as the screen comes (`autoFocus`), and its focus says where the list went, or which one was removed.
- F44, finishing a list and opening it again (its "ביטול", "פתיחה מחדש", "פתיחה מחדש לכולם"): the screen starts anew each time, so the frame keeps what was pressed (`arrival`, set at the press, forgotten once the new screen took it or the press was refused). After a finish "ביטול" takes the focus, which says the list closed (its row `quiet` then, so the announcer doesn't say it too); once open again, the list's title has the focus and the announcer says "הרשימה נפתחה שוב". With the focus put elsewhere meanwhile, the words are said and the focus stays.
- F54, on phones: "נסגרו היום" on Lists and "מה קרה ברשימה" on a list are headings too, each an `sr-only` h2 just before its `<details>`, phone only, so the look is the same and the wider screens keep their own section headings. Not a heading inside the summary: the summary reads as a button, which some screen readers flatten, the phones' VoiceOver among them.
- Tests: unit `access-undo` (its time, the wait and what it had left, an earlier row's wait changing nothing of the next, cleared; the selector) and `editor-focus` (the selector, quotes, backslashes and new lines included; the group's row at its place; where a sheet closes onto). e2e: the new `manage-lists-a11y.spec.ts` on the twins (who sees pay by the keyboard past its time and by a mouse after it, a role's undo by Tab; the scheduling settings' position and shift, Escape back on the row, the removal not said by the announcer; the template editor's item and time; the phone headings at every width); `design.spec.ts` (the sent list's "ביטול" focused on Lists, the photo's "ביטול" and the remove button after it); `lists.spec.ts` (the real finish's "ביטול" focused and not said twice, the list's title after taking it back and the announcer's words, and again after the second finish); `list-templates.spec.ts` (the real removed template's "ביטול" focused).
- Docs: ROADMAP known issue 105 without who sees pay, removing from a sheet, finishing a list and the phone headings; the accessibility row's part 8.
- Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright (CI is the first run of the new e2e), pgTAP, real screen readers.
- Defaults applied, to ask Orel: (1) who sees pay keeps its "ביטול" under the list, focused and waiting, rather than the roles screen's toast at the top; (2) a role's undo waits when reached too, as the person's does, though it holds no write; (3) after a finish is taken back, the focus goes to the list's title, not its first item still to do; (4) the phone headings are only for screen readers, just before what they fold, not inside the summary; (5) a photo's "ביטול" takes the focus only where the remove button still had it, or nothing had it.
- After review: a removal from a sheet whose "ביטול" is out of view (it comes under the whole list, and the page stays where the row was: a long list, a short screen) has it brought into view as the Sheet focuses it (`useSheetReturn`'s `returnFocus` wraps it in `inViewWhenFocused`, only when it is the one chosen). A refused way back gives its "ביטול" the focus again once for each refusal, once the button no longer waits (`refusalArrived` in `manage/editor-focus.ts`, by `arrived`), so a list screen shown again from a hidden <Activity> no longer takes the focus and moves the page to a photo's "ביטול" refused long before; Lists right after sending and the templates right after removing one do the same when taking back is refused (their "ביטול" was disabled while it waited, and nothing gave the focus back), the templates' only for the undo's own press (`failure.fromUndo`). A photo's buttons are found inside the item's own row: `ChecklistItem` takes `itemId`, on its root as `data-list-item` (`LIST_ITEM`; `packages/ui`, and on `/design` with the gallery's photo), so of two items with the same label the focus no longer goes to the other one. A finish's or an opening's `arrival` acts only on a screen whose list is as it says (finished only when not open, opened again only when open), so a press whose screen was left before its answer no longer says "הרשימה נפתחה שוב" on a list finished again since. A position's "ביטול" takes the focus as it appears only for the removal just pressed, or for a refused undo while the focus is lost or still on that position's trash button; the earlier way back that a refused removal shows again takes none (the removed row gives the focus back to the trash button), so a slow refusal no longer takes the focus from the field the person went on to. Tests: unit `editor-focus` (`refusalArrived`: once for each, not while it waits, nothing for none) and `checklist-item` (the id on the root of every kind, none without one); e2e: the template editor on a short screen, its first item removed from its sheet onto "ביטול" focused and in view; the scheduling twin's undo refused while the person names a new position, the focus kept in that field; the lists twin's taking back refused (the request dropped), "ביטול" focused again. Left as it was: an `arrival` has no time with it, so one left by a press whose answer never came still acts on a later screen of a list in that state (the design twin's changes answer ok and change nothing, so its arrival waits until `?state=` changes); no e2e for a photo's undo refused and the screen shown again from a hidden <Activity>, nor for leaving a list before a finish's answer (the guards are `refusalArrived`, tested, and one comparison).
- After the check: a shift's undo refused once the removal was stored took the row away again (the screen goes back to what is stored, read again since the removal) with the focus it had, which fell to the body, and the way back shown again was `quiet`, so nothing said it: `removed` notes `refused`, the row is quiet only when the sheet brings the focus to it, and a layout effect gives a focus lost to its "ביטול" as it is drawn, once for each refusal (`refusalArrived`), so its focus says it and the announcer doesn't; where the person went on, or with the row still there (the twin, whose stored values never change), the focus stays and the row says itself. A finish or an opening again refused with the screen kept (no connection, a slip) left the focus on the body after its disabled wait: `finish` and `reopen` note what had the focus as it was pressed (the finish, its "ביטול", "פתיחה מחדש", "פתיחה מחדש לכולם") and hold the refusal (`pressRefused`) until nothing is on its way, then give the focus back to it where it was lost, and only then say why (the toast), as part 5 does; the refusals that read the list again (`movesOn`, now shared with `fail`) move no focus. A photo's undo refused said its words twice, the row's own words changing as its "ביטול" took the focus again: the row keeps "התמונה הוסרה", which that focus says, and "התמונה לא חזרה. אפשר לנסות שוב." is a line of its own under it (`role="alert"`), drawn once the focus is back (`photoUndoSaid`) and afresh for each refusal. The phone headings stay as they are, and default (4) has this with it: read in order, each title is said twice (the heading, then the summary), and VoiceOver's headings land on a heading that opens nothing; the WAI-ARIA disclosure (an h2 around a button with `aria-expanded`, a client part on Lists) would say it once, if Orel prefers it. origin/main b7ef204 (#251) merged in: the accessibility row carries parts 5 and 8, and known issue 105 keeps both parts' removals; its `ignoreHeldEnter` on UndoRow keeps the "ביטול" rows focused here from an Enter held down on what removed. The first bullet names ChecklistItem's `itemId`, the one change in `packages/ui`. Tests, e2e: `manage.spec.ts` on the real `/manage/scheduling` (a shift's undo dropped while its row has the focus: the row gone, "ביטול" focused and described, the announcer silent); `design.spec.ts` (the photo's undo refused: the focus back, the row's words kept, the line said after the focus and never by the row; the twin's finish taken back and refused: the focus back before the toast). Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright, pgTAP.
- After the second review: a finish or an opening again refused because someone else closed or opened the list meanwhile said nothing: its answer reads the list again, so the screen starts anew in the same moment the press stops waiting, and the refusal held on the old screen (`pressRefused`) went with it. The frame holds it too now (`arrival` takes `{ refused }`, by `refusedPress`): the screen read again puts a focus lost on the list's title and only then says why ("הרשימה כבר נסגרה.", from the frame's toast), and a screen still shown once nothing is on its way says it as before and forgets the frame's; there a refusal that reads the list again (`movesOn`) puts a focus lost on the title rather than leaving it on the body. A shift's removal refused before its sheet had finished closing (offline it is refused at once) closed the sheet onto an earlier removal's "ביטול", which said that other shift went, or onto the section's title: `useSheetReturn` has `kept`, which the shifts editor calls on a refused removal with the row it put back (`groupRow`, read once the sheet has gone, since that row came back as another), and `sheetReturnTo` takes it (`kept`) before the title, brought into view; refused once the sheet had gone, the "ביטול" that goes gives that row the focus, as before. An UndoRow whose own focus says its words (`autoFocus`, `quiet`, `buttonRef`: `namedByWords`) has them in its button's name, after its word ("ביטול: הרשימה נסגרה", `ui.undoRow.named`), and no description: VoiceOver says a description only after a pause, cut off by the next swipe, and never with its hints off. A row with none of the three keeps its description (a `buttonRef` alone names it, even where the announcer says the row as it appears: the screen that holds it brings the focus there; corrected after the third check). This is UndoRow's, so every such row in the app has it, the earlier parts' too, and their checks of the description now check the name (`undoNamed`, `UNDO_NAMED` in `e2e/t.ts`). After sending and on the templates, a refused undo's line (`role="alert"`) came in before "ביטול" had the focus back, and the focus's move cut it off: it is drawn once the focus is back (`said`, as the photo's `photoUndoSaid`), on the templates only for the undo's own press; a folded row's refusal moves no focus and is said at once. Their "ביטול" took the focus whenever the row was drawn (a reload, the address opened again within the five minutes, a `?removed=` address at any time): it takes it only right after the press that left for it (`lists/press-arrival.ts`: the send form and the template editor call `leavingFor` just before they go, the notice reads `cameFromPress` as it draws and `arrivalTaken` once drawn, kept in the page's memory, so a reload forgets it); otherwise the focus stays and the announcer says the row. Who sees pay's six seconds are one constant, `ACCESS_UNDO_MS` in `access-undo.ts`, which the screen and its checks share. Tests: unit `undo-row` (named, with no description, for each of the three, in both languages; `namedByWords`), `editor-focus` (`sheetReturnTo` onto the row kept, never an earlier "ביטול"), `press-arrival` (read as often as it draws until taken, none for another list or kind, one at a time). e2e: `lists.spec.ts` (a finish refused as Shiran closed the list from her screen: the title focused, then the toast's words); `manage-lists-a11y.spec.ts` (a shift's removal dropped at once with an earlier removal's way back up: the shift's row focused, not that "ביטול"; a time removed from its sheet not said by the announcer; Lists and the templates reached by their address: "ביטול" not focused, and said by the announcer; the templates' undo refused: "ביטול" focused again before its line, and a folded row's refused restore leaving the focus off it; the access checks wait on `ACCESS_UNDO_MS`); `design.spec.ts` (the sent list's taking back refused: its line after the focus); and the name in place of the description in `lists`, `manage`, `manage-lists-a11y`, `design`, `team-a11y`, `team-changes`, `availability` and `builder-focus`. Defaults applied, to ask Orel: (6) every "ביטול" whose focus says what happened says it in its name, in every part; (7) a refused finish or opening again that reads the list again puts the focus on the list's title; (8) a way back reached by its address, not right after its press, takes no focus. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright, pgTAP, real screen readers.
- After the third check: a shift's removal refused once its sheet had gone, with another shift's sheet opened meanwhile, closed that sheet onto the refused shift's row: `useSheetReturn` keeps one `sheetReturn` for the screen now (no React, so the tests stand in for the page), `removing` gives each removal its number, and `kept` takes it and acts only while that removal's sheet still closes (true), so a refusal after that changes no later sheet's way back (false: the "ביטול" that goes gives the row the focus, as before). A shift's removal refused said "השינוי לא נשמר" before the focus reached the row, and the move cut it off: the settings' `change` takes `hold`, and its card waits (`held`) for `sayHeld` (the shifts editor's `sayRefusal`), called a moment after the sheet's close has given the row the focus (`kept`'s `landed`, queued by `returnFocus`) or, with the sheet gone already, queued after the focus the "ביטול" that goes gives the row (`goneRefusal`, a layout effect of that drawing); a kept row whose close won't come (another sheet opened, another removal) says it at once. A position's removal refused brought an earlier removal's way back again as a new row the announcer said ("התפקיד ... הוסר", about another position, long after the fact): it comes back `quiet`, and UndoRow's `quiet` says so (a row that was up already, drawn again, is no news). A folded template's "החזרה" refused left the focus on the body after its disabled wait: it has the focus back (`data-template-restore`, `TEMPLATE_RESTORE`), then the line says why, as the top undo does. Taking back a sent list refused because someone started it drew "כבר התחילו למלא את הרשימה" as its row went, and the focus's move to the page's title cut it off: the line is drawn once the title has the focus (`said`), now `role="alert"` as the slip's line is, since a status drawn with its words in it is often not heard. UndoRow's docs said its button is described: `packages/ui/README.md` and its `buttonRef` now say what the code does (named when `autoFocus`, `quiet` or `buttonRef` is set, so a `buttonRef` alone names a row the announcer says too), and the second review's line above is corrected. Tests: unit `use-sheet-return` (a removal's sheet onto its "ביטול", any other onto what opened it; refused while it closes, onto the row put back and said after; refused after, another sheet onto its own row; an earlier removal refused during a later one's close; a kept row whose close won't come said at once) and `undo-row` (a `buttonRef` alone names it). e2e: `manage-lists-a11y.spec.ts` (the shift's refusal while its sheet closes said with the focus on its row; refused once its sheet has gone, the save held and then dropped, said once its row has the focus; another shift's sheet opened meanwhile closing onto its own row; a position's refused removal not saying the earlier way back again; the folded "החזרה" refused, focused before its line); `lists.spec.ts` (taking back refused as Noa started the list: the title focused before the line). Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright, pgTAP, real screen readers.
- Next: the reviewers; CI's e2e; the sibling part of lists-me-file and the other per-screen parts.

## 2026-11-25 · chat · the agent log split by month

- `docs/agent/LOG.md` had grown past 1 MiB (1.12 MB, 170 entries), over Biome's file size limit, so every `pnpm check` warned and skipped it, and a run catching up read all of it. The entries from 20 November 2026 on stay here (48, about 400 KB); the 98 from 1 to 19 November moved to `docs/agent/log/2026-11-01-to-19.md` and the 24 from October to `docs/agent/log/2026-10.md`, word for word and in the same order, newest on top. A line under this file's header points there.
- `docs/AGENT.md` (catching up, and where the log is) and `README.md` name the folder. An entry's own references to `docs/agent/LOG.md` are left as they were written; a migration's comment is never edited.
- Checked: every heading and every entry found once across the three files; `pnpm check`.
- Defaults applied, to ask Orel: the split is by month, at 20 November, so this file stays under half its limit for a while; when it nears 1 MiB again, the next month moves the same way.

## 2026-11-24 · chat · accessibility, part 7: chat and documents

- On chat/a11y-chat from origin/main fde02d8 (#247). The sweep's chat-docs findings 32, 33, 36, 37 and 38, with the shared parts from #243; 34 and 35 came with #243. No migration, no new package, no SQL, and the actions answer as before. In `packages/ui`: MessageBubble's `actions` take a `fullLabel`, and Composer draws both of its shapes in one wrapper with a live line for why writing stopped, each on `/design` (the gallery's bubble with its actions, and the composer where nobody can write, which sending there now closes, with a button that closes and opens it: `design.chatClosedText`, `design.chatClose`, and a sentence on the button's name in `design.chatActionsText`).
- F32, a chat opened from the list beside it (tablet and desktop): `app/(app)/chat/chat-focus.ts` now builds on `focusLost` from `@ui/focus-after` (the chat screens' callers unchanged, a disabled control or a sheet's frame counting as lost too) and adds `titleTakesFocus` (lost, or still on what opened the screen in the list, marked `data-chat-list` on InboxPane's root: the open chat's row, or "שליחת הודעה" marked `CHAT_OPENER`, as after review) and `screenTitle` (the page's title beside the list, never the list's own). The conversation's title takes the focus as it opens where `titleTakesFocus` says so, without moving the page, and so does ChatPageHeader's (the announcements beside the list too). "שליחת הודעה" gives Sheet a `returnFocus`: with the chat already on screen chosen again, its title; with another chosen, its own button, marked (`CHAT_OPENER`) when nothing took the focus as the sheet closed, which the chosen chat takes the focus from as it opens; closed with no one chosen, its button (as after review).
- F33, a text message that didn't go: where no line over the field says why (`tooFast`, `businessChanged`) and the chat didn't close (`closed`, `noAccess`, which draw the screen again with the reason), the conversation's own line says `app.chat.conversation.notSent`, "ההודעה לא נשלחה. אפשר לנסות שוב מתחת להודעה" ("The message wasn't sent. You can try again under it."), each time, a retry's too. Composer: the reason nobody can write stands in the same wrapper as the form (what floats over it stays), and when it comes while the composer is drawn, the line takes the focus that was in the form (`watchFocusIn`, `focusFirst`, no page move); with the focus elsewhere, an always-present polite line says it there, for seven seconds (`ANNOUNCE_CLEAR_MS`). Drawn so from the start, nothing is said.
- F36, the desktop's documents table: closing the preview puts the focus on the chosen row's button first (`th button[aria-current="true"]`, `focusChosenRow`, brought into view only when a key closed it, as after the check), then clears the choice. Each change of the filters says through the announcer how many documents are left, `app.chat.documents.office.filters.count` ("{count, plural, =0 {אין מסמכים שמתאימים} one {מסמך אחד} other {# מסמכים}}", "No documents match / One document / # documents"), once per change and never on load: once a person's documents came with the address (`filtersRead`), and once the menu gave its button the focus back (`whenFocused`: never while a menu has the focus, as after the check; at most 600 ms), so the focus doesn't cut the words off (`app/(app)/chat/documents/filter-count.ts`). "ניקוי הסינון" goes with the filters it clears: its own component with `useFocusWhenGone`, the focus on to the first filter ("שם: כולם").
- F37, the send form: the "לבקש אישור קריאה" switch is described by its hint (FieldRow `hintId`, the Switch's `aria-describedby`); the sick note's line is the category list's live `note`, in its own weight as before, so it is read with the list and said as "אישור מחלה" is chosen.
- F38, each message's actions: the button's name starts with "פעולות" and says whose message, when and how it starts, and its tooltip is that name (as after review), `app.chat.messageActions.openFor` "פעולות להודעה של {name} {when, select, today {מ-<ltr>{time}</ltr>} yesterday {מאתמול ב-<ltr>{time}</ltr>} other {מ-<ltr>{date}</ltr> ב-<ltr>{time}</ltr>}}: {words}" and `openForOwn` the same with "שלך" for "של {name}" ("Actions for the message from {name} at …: {words}", "Actions for your message at …: {words}"), the time in Unicode isolates (`t.markup`), in the app and its twin. A name, not `aria-describedby` on the author and the time: a list of the page's buttons (VoiceOver's rotor, TalkBack's controls) reads names only, which is where the buttons couldn't be told apart.
- Tests: unit `chat-focus.test.ts` (lost, in the list, elsewhere; the title beside the list, a hidden one skipped) and `filter-count.test.ts` (whose documents are read, the wait for the focus, its timeout and stop). e2e: new `chat-focus.spec.ts` on the gallery and the twins (the composer's line focused after a send that closes the chat and said with the focus elsewhere; the bubble's actions name and tooltip; the documents table's row focused after the preview, the counts said with the focus on the menu's button, none on load, the focus on the first filter after clearing, Noa's three after her address; the send form's descriptions and the sick note said), and in the real flows `chat.spec.ts` (a send dropped on its way, so nothing is written, said with the field still focused, and the kitchen's two messages' actions names; in the tablet's shape, a row pressed by the keyboard and a chat chosen in "שליחת הודעה" each focus the title, and Escape gives the sheet's button the focus back). Nothing new is written or sent beyond what those specs already do (opening chats marks them read; `clearDemoChat` puts that back).
- Docs: ROADMAP known issue 105 without the chat and documents group; the accessibility row's part 7.
- Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright (CI is the first run of the new e2e), pgTAP, real screen readers.
- Defaults applied, to ask Orel: (1) the actions button's whole name says whose message, when and how it starts, rather than a description, and its tooltip is that name (as after review); (2) a message that didn't go because the chat closed says only why writing stopped, not "אפשר לנסות שוב"; (3) the line that says why nobody can write takes the focus only when the focus was in the composer, and is otherwise said once; (4) after "ניקוי הסינון" the focus goes to the first filter; (5) a person's count waits for their documents to come with the address; (6) the announcements' title beside the list takes the focus as a conversation's does; (7) closing the documents preview brings the chosen row into view only when a key closed it, and a mouse's click leaves the page where it was scrolled to (after the check).
- After review (seven findings, each confirmed; two were one gap): (1) a chat screen took the focus from anywhere in the list beside it, a row the person moved to while it loaded included: `titleTakesFocus` now takes it from the list only on what opened the screen, the row marked as the chat open (ChatRow's and ListRow's `aria-current`, there once the address is the chat's) or "שליחת הודעה" marked `data-chat-opener` (`CHAT_OPENER`) while the chat chosen in it opens; the mark goes once the button loses the focus. (2, 6) "שליחת הודעה" closed onto whatever title was beside the list, the chat being left or a skeleton's "טוען", which has no tabindex (the focus then fell to the page): now the title only when the one chosen is the chat on screen (the address at the press, `chose` "again"), else its own button, marked when nothing took the focus as the sheet closed, and the chosen chat takes it from there as it opens; `screenTitle` skips a title with no tabindex. (3) The actions button's tooltip stayed "פעולות" under the longer name, so it was read again as its description: the tooltip is the name itself now. (4) The name repeated for one person's run of messages in a minute, and for the same time on another day: `openFor` and `openForOwn` say the day when it isn't today's and how the message starts, "פעולות להודעה של {name} {when, select, today {מ-<ltr>{time}</ltr>} yesterday {מאתמול ב-<ltr>{time}</ltr>} other {מ-<ltr>{date}</ltr> ב-<ltr>{time}</ltr>}}: {words}" ("Actions for the message from {name} at …, yesterday at …, on … at …: {words}"), its first 40 characters as its actions sheet quotes it (`wordsOf`: a picture or a PDF in words), from `actionsNameValues` (`app/(app)/chat/actions-name.ts`) in the app, its twin and the gallery. This turns default (1) around: the tooltip is no longer "פעולות" alone. (5) The gallery's button said "לסגור את השיחה" over a chat already closed: its words follow the state, `design.chatOpen` "לפתוח את השיחה" ("Open the chat") while closed, and no `aria-pressed`. (7) The tablet's e2e pressed a row before the kitchen's focus effect had surely run: it waits for the kitchen's title to have the focus first. Tests: unit `chat-focus.test.ts` (the current row and the marked button give it, another row and the unmarked button keep it, a skeleton's title skipped) and new `actions-name.test.ts` (a run in one minute reads apart; yesterday's, another date's and another year's; the cut; in English); e2e `chat-focus.spec.ts` (the gallery's button by its words in each state; the actions tooltip equal to the name, no "פעולות" description) and `chat.spec.ts` (the kitchen's names with their day and words, from each message's own time; in the tablet's shape, the announcements held on their way while the kitchen's row is moved to, which keeps the focus; Yoav chosen with his new chat held, "שליחת הודעה" focused until his title takes it; Shiran chosen again, her title; `holdScreen` holds a navigation's answer, never a prefetch). Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright, `pnpm build`.
- After the check (three findings, each confirmed): (1) a count from the category or state menu was said while its item still had the focus, so the focus going back to the menu's button cut it off, and the forms step of `chat-focus.spec.ts` couldn't pass: Radix makes a menu's choice in `flushSync` before it closes the menu, and React runs that commit's effects at once, so the count's effect ran with the focus in the open menu, which `focusLost` doesn't count as lost. `whenFocused` now waits while the focus is in a menu (`[role="menu"]`, its items and itself), and on each move of the focus says it only once it is neither lost nor in a menu; the 600 ms stays as the last resort. The person filter and "ניקוי הסינון" passed before only by their own waits. (2) Closing the preview brought the chosen row back into view for a mouse's click too, moving the page of someone who had scrolled the table on: `focusChosenRow` (`filter-count.ts`) brings it into view only when a key closed it (the click's `detail` 0), and a mouse's click leaves the page where it was, as `focusWhenGone` does; default (7). (3) The F32 and F38 bullets and default (1) still described the code before review: they now say the `returnFocus` and `titleTakesFocus` as they are, the `openFor` key with its day and words, and the tooltip as the name. Tests: unit `filter-count.test.ts` (a menu's item keeps the count waiting, another item too, said once its button has the focus; said after the wait where a menu keeps the focus; the chosen row focused from the preview's button, brought into view for a key and not for a mouse); the e2e as it was, its forms step now able to pass. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright, `pnpm build`.
- After the second review (eight findings, each confirmed): (1) Composer's "writing stopped" compared with what it knew from before its screen was hidden for going back (an `<Activity>`): a chat closed meanwhile and opened again from the list gave the closed line the focus the field had when the person left (on a phone, so the title then took none), or said the reason (from tablet up). A layout effect's cleanup, run on a hide and on unmount, forgets the last drawing (`wasWriting` null, `hadFocus` false), so a screen shown again is drawn so from the start. (2) A send refused because the reader no longer reads the chat (`noAccess`: their department removed, or they were taken out of it), or with chat turned off, draws a not-found screen with no Composer, and the focus fell to the page with nothing said: the app's not-found screen and the chat's use `NotFoundState` (`app/(app)/not-found-state.tsx`), whose title takes the focus when nothing holds it as it comes (`focusFirst`, no page move). (3) "לא נשלחה. לנסות שוב" in a chat already closed failed again in silence (nothing on screen changes, and the Composer speaks only on a change): a `closed` or `noAccess` answer to a send that went while the screen showed the chat closed says `app.chat.conversation.notSentBlocked`, "ההודעה לא נשלחה, כי אי אפשר לכתוב כאן" ("The message wasn't sent, because you can't write here."). Pictures and PDFs were left out of it (after the third check, below). (4) The desktop's documents table, always drawn and hidden below 1280px, kept its person filter on "כולם" after a person was chosen in the tablet's list (an iPad turned on its side), and no count was said again: the filter follows an address its menus didn't ask for (`asked`), unsaid. (5) The PR body's F38 bullet gave the name without the day and the words: a corrected body is ready, not posted (the repository is public, so it waits for Orel's yes). (6, 7, 8) Tests only: a chat closing while someone writes, in the app and not only on the gallery; the announcements' title taking the focus from its own row; the gallery's composer drawn closed saying nothing on load. Defaults applied, to ask Orel: (8) the app's not-found title takes the focus wherever nothing holds it, not only after a chat; (9) a retry in a closed chat says it didn't go and that nobody can write here, rather than the reason's own words. Tests: e2e `chat.spec.ts` (Shiran writes as the kitchen is removed: the closed line focused, neither `notSent` nor the reason said, a retry from the keyboard says `notSentBlocked`, nothing written; Noa the same, the not-found title focused; Shiran's kitchen removed while kept for going back, opened again from the list with its title focused and nothing said; in the tablet's shape the kitchen's row pressed again, then the announcements' row, each title focused; `closeDemoDepartment` in `e2e/db.ts` removes a demo department and returns what brings it back) and `chat-focus.spec.ts` (nothing said as the gallery loads; the documents twin at 820px with Noa chosen in its list, then at 1440px the menu reads her and her sick notes are counted, nothing said for the hidden table before). Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright, `pnpm build`.
- After the third check (four findings, two of them the same one; each fixed, none left as not real): (1, 2) a picture or a PDF tried again from its bubble in a chat already closed still failed in silence: `deliver` hands them to `deliverPicture` and `deliverPdf` before the line that said `notSentBlocked`, their refusals for `closed` and `noAccess` carry no notice, and the Composer draws none while nobody writes anyway. One helper, `refusedWhileBlocked`, now says it for all three kinds: from the text's branch, after `pictureRefusal`, and after `runPdfJob`'s end. The second review's "Pictures and PDFs keep their own notices" was wrong for this case, and says so now. (3) An announcement refused as no longer the writer's (`noAccess`: "הודעה מההנהלה" taken from their role between drawing and sending) left the chat drawn again still open, only without the switch, with nothing said, and each retry was refused the same way: the screen keeps such drafts until it is drawn again, and drawn without the switch and still open to write in, it says `app.chat.conversation.notSentAnnouncement`, "ההודעה לא נשלחה, כי כבר אין לך הרשאה לפרסם הודעות מההנהלה. אפשר לנסות שוב מתחת להודעה, והיא תישלח כהודעה רגילה" ("The message wasn't sent, because you're no longer allowed to post messages from management. You can try again under it, and it goes as a regular message."), and they stay as messages, which their retry sends. Drawn closed to them, the Composer says why as before; in place of a not-found screen nothing more is said. (4) From tablet up, the chat's not-found left the focus on the list's row (or "שליחת הודעה") that opened it, so nothing said the screen beside changed: `NotFoundState` takes an optional `takesFocus`, asked once as it comes (by default, only a lost focus), and the chat's not-found draws it through `ChatNotFoundState` (`app/(app)/chat/chat-not-found-state.tsx`) with `titleTakesFocus`, so its title takes the focus where a chat's would. The app's own not-found is as it was. Default applied, to ask Orel: (10) an announcement no longer the writer's to make stays as a regular message, sent by its retry, rather than an announcement that can never go. Tests: e2e `chat.spec.ts` (in the closed kitchen, a picture dropped on its way while it was open, tried again from the keyboard there: `notSentBlocked` said again, nothing written; in the tablet's shape, the list's syncs answering nothing new, the removed kitchen's row pressed gives Noa's not-found title the focus) and `chat-announce.spec.ts` (Shiran's announcement refused once her role lost it: the switch gone, `notSentAnnouncement` said, no announcement's label on it, its retry written as a message; `setDemoManagersAnnounce` and `demoChatKind` in `e2e/db.ts`). Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright, `pnpm build`.
- Next: the reviewers; CI's e2e; the other per-screen parts.

## 2026-11-24 · chat · accessibility, part 5: the clock and the hours

- On chat/a11y-clock from origin/main 76bb20c (#248), with origin/main fde02d8 (#247) merged in after the check. The sweep's clock-team findings 24, 25, 26, 29, 30 and 31 (deleting an invite, the invite form and the departments are another part). No migration, no new package, nothing in `packages/ui`, and the server actions answer as before. One twin state more: `/design/shell/clock/code?other=prep`, the device already the business's kitchen's screen, its stop and "ביטול" kept in the page (`OtherStationCode`, nothing stored).
- F24, `/clock`: a press of the clock's own buttons ("יציאה להפסקה", "יציאה מהמשמרת", the circle, "ביטול היציאה", and the press without a code) is noted with the state it was pressed in (`clockState`, `ClockPress` in `clock/clock-focus.ts`). Once nothing is on its way and the screen it led to is drawn, or it was refused (`pressOver`), the focus goes, only where it was lost, back to what was pressed if it is still there (a refusal, the circle), else to the screen's main control: "יציאה מהמשמרת" during a break, the circle otherwise, whose name says the time running and what pressing it does. Enter held down presses nothing more on these buttons (`ignoreHeldEnter`, a key's repeat), so the break's start never goes on to end the shift once the focus has moved there.
- F25: PunchCodeSheet and WithoutCodeSheet pass Sheet a `returnFocus`, and ClockPanel gives both its main control: after the digits, after "כניסה בלי קוד", or closed by ✕ or Escape, the focus is on the circle (or "יציאה מהמשמרת" during a break), never the top of the page. Back to the camera from a sheet, the camera's own "ביטול" keeps it, as before.
- F26, `/clock/team`: each waiting entry is a row of its own that watches its focus (`useFocusWhenGone`). An approved entry leaves the card at once, until the list is read again without it (as StationsCard keeps its stopped rows), and the focus goes on to the next entry's words down, else the nearest one up (`afterApproval` in `clock/team/next-approval.ts`, with part 3's `nextInLine`; `data-confirm-target` on the name and what waits), else the card's title, else the page's once the last one took the card along. The toast names whose hours were approved, after the focus has landed: `app.clock.team.confirm.done` is now "השעות של {name} אושרו" / "{name}'s hours approved" (no form of address: the verb follows "השעות").
- F29, the hours card on the history, the team's hours and a person's hours (`MonthHero`): each month is a page of its own (the month is in the address, and Next keeps the page before hidden), so the arrow pressed went with its page on every press, not only on reaching this month. A client row (`MonthRow`, `clock/month-row.tsx`) notes a plain press of an arrow (`data-month-way`, `createMonthPresses` in `clock/month-press.ts`, kept 15 seconds), and the month it leads to takes it: the same arrow there has the focus, so it can be pressed again, else the month's title (this month has no "חודש הבא"), and then the month and its hours are said through the page's announcer (`app.clock.history.monthShown`, "{month}: {hours} {caption}"). Once only: the same month shown again later says nothing. The pay screens' arrows (PayHero) are as they were.
- F30, a person's hours: the toast's "ביטול" keeps its word and says what it brings back (StatusToast's `fullLabel`): "ביטול: המשמרת חוזרת" after a cancel, "ביטול: ההפסקה חוזרת" after a break removed, "ביטול: ההיעדרות חוזרת" after days away removed (`app.clock.team.done.undoCancel`, `undoRemoveBreak`, `app.clock.absences.done.undoRemove`, with English twins). Days away removed: once the toast goes holding the focus, the days away's title has it (the row went with the removal); after its "ביטול", the title has it while the record comes back, and then the record's row (`AbsencesSection`'s `restored` and `headingRef`, the row found by its place on the shape drawn), only where the focus is still there or lost.
- F31, the clock's lock on a device that is the kitchen's screen or the till (`LockStation`): once the stop's toast goes holding the focus, "נעילת המכשיר על הקוד" has it; after its "ביטול" too, and when the other one runs again and the lock goes, the stop back in its place (`WatchedButton` with `useFocusWhenGone`), else the line saying what the device is, else the page's title.
- Tests: unit `clock-focus` (the state's key, when a press is over, a held Enter), `month-press` (taken once, by its own month and list, only the last one, not when late), `next-approval` (down, then up, then the title; the selector's escaping), `confirm-card` (each entry's words marked, the card's title). e2e: `clock.spec.ts` (from the keyboard: the circle, "יציאה להפסקה" held down with the focus then on "יציאה מהמשמרת" and nothing more pressed, off the clock, "ביטול היציאה" back to the break, back to work, out and back from the circle, each time the main control focused; a month back by the keyboard with its arrow focused and the month's words said, on to this month by a mouse with its title focused), `clock-code.spec.ts` (the digits' sheet and "כניסה בלי קוד" close onto the circle, ✕ too, a break onto "יציאה מהמשמרת"; the last approval's focus on the page's title and its named toast said after it), `clock-team.spec.ts` (the cancel's and the break's "ביטול" named for what comes back), `absences.spec.ts` (the removal's "ביטול" focused and named, the record's row focused once back), `design.spec.ts` (the twin's two approvals: the next entry's words, then the page's title, each toast said after the focus; the twin's days away: named, the row after "ביטול", the title once the toast's time is up; the lock twin: the lock after the toast's time, the stop after its "ביטול"; the cancel's and the break's names; the new twin state's clean check).
- Docs: ROADMAP known issue 105 without the break and leaving, the clock's sheets, approving hours, changing month, "ביטול" after a cancel and the lock station.
- Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright (CI is the first run of the new e2e), pgTAP, real screen readers.
- Defaults applied, to ask Orel: during a break the clock's focus goes to "יציאה מהמשמרת" (the button in the break's place), not the circle's "חזרה לעבודה"; after leaving from a break, to the circle, not the time-limited "ביטול היציאה" card under it; a held Enter on the clock's buttons presses once. After an approval the focus goes to the next entry's words, not its "אישור" (Enter held down would approve it too, as on the swaps), and the approved entry leaves the card before the screen is read again. Changing month keeps the focus on the same arrow, not the month's title, so it can be pressed again; the words are "אוקטובר 2026: 12:30 שעות, 5 משמרות". One name for the way back from any days away ("ביטול: ההיעדרות חוזרת", as the toast says "ההיעדרות הוסרה"), not one per kind.
- After review: on `/clock`, what a press says now comes after the focus has landed, never before (a focus moved as words are said cuts them off): the toast's words, and a refusal's alert, are held with the press and said by the effect that moves the focus once it is over (`sayWhenOver`, `held`). A code's punch that went through (scanned or typed) is noted as a press too, so its toast waits until the sheet or the camera gave the focus back, and a focus left on "יציאה מהמשמרת" as the digits' sheet closed during a break goes on to the circle once the page read again removes it. "ביטול היציאה" watches its focus (`UndoLeaving`, `useFocusWhenGone`): gone once its time is up, the main control has it. `data-clock` marks the circle and "יציאה מהמשמרת" for the tests. The lock station: the stop is watched as the lock is (`WatchedButton`), so a stop with no way back gives the focus to the lock it opened, and Enter held down presses neither (`ignoreHeldEnter`); a stop with no way back and the restart's "פועל שוב" are said a microtask after the screen they led to is drawn, after the focus's move (`toastOnceMoved`). Days away brought back: "ההיעדרות חזרה" is said once the row has the focus (`restored.shown`). Approving hours: a refusal gives the focus back to the "אישור" pressed (busy meanwhile, so it lost it), else the card's title, and then says why. Changing month onto the month's title says only the hours (`app.clock.history.monthHours`, "{hours} {caption}"), as the title says the month. The pay screens' arrows (PayHero, each month a page of its own, drawn in several shapes) stay as they were, and known issue 105 lists them again ("החלפת חודש בשכר"). Tests: `noteFocusWhenSaid` also hears an alert and can ask for the focused element's words; `clock.spec.ts` checks each press's toast is said with the focus already on the circle or "יציאה מהמשמרת", and the month's title with the hours alone; `clock-code.spec.ts` leaves from a break by the digits ("יציאה מהמשמרת", then the circle) and checks the sheets' toasts after the focus; `absences.spec.ts` the restore's words after the row; `design.spec.ts` the undo card running out (the page's clock run on), the lock twin with Enter held down and the restart said after the stop's focus, a new twin state `/design/shell/clock/code?other=prep&restart=off` (a stop with no way back), a refused approval with the network dropped, and the days away twin's title before the row (the stand-in keeps the row, so the step between is what shows it).
- After the check (four findings): (1) the branch conflicted with origin/main fde02d8 (#247, part 4) on known issue 105, and the log would have put part 4's entry above this one: origin/main is merged in (a merge commit, as the sibling parts did; nothing rebased), 105 is main's line with only the clock's clause changed ("בשעון ובצוות (מחיקת הזמנה, הזמנה, מחלקות, החלפת חודש בשכר)", no "בהיום ובסידור" any more), and this entry stays on top. (2) After a code's punch through a sheet (the digits, or "כניסה בלי קוד" when the page read again came within the sheet's 220 ms), the press's effect put the focus on the circle while the sheet was still sliding away, with the rest of the page still under Radix's `aria-hidden` (only live regions spared): VoiceOver in Safari might say nothing of it, and nothing would say it again. Chromium lets it pass. The effect now waits while the focus is in a closing sheet (`inClosingSheet`, from main's `@ui/focus-after`), and both sheets' `returnFocus` (`sheetGaveBack`) counts the hand-back (`handedBack`), which runs the effect again once the sheet has gone and the focus is on the main control: then only the held words. (3) The F31 bullet named `OpenLock`; it is `WatchedButton`. (4) The clock's twin (`/design/shell/clock`) moves each stand-in to its state's own address, a page of its own that Next keeps hidden after it, so the focus after a press can't show there: the twin's page comment says it shows only on `/clock`, the hosted demo's included. The twin is as it was. Tests: e2e `clock-code.spec.ts` (with the motion a phone has, for the digits in, the digits out from a break and "כניסה בלי קוד": nothing takes the focus while hidden, `noteHiddenFocus`, beside the circle focused and the toast said after it). Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright, `pnpm build`, VoiceOver in Safari.
- After the second review (thirteen findings, all kept): `packages/ui` changes after all, in two components already on `/design`. (1, 3) Enter held down on the lock station's stop, or on a person's hours' cancel or break removal, went on to press the toast's "ביטול" that took the focus in their place (`focusAction`), so the stop or the cancel was taken back at once: StatusToast's action now takes no key repeating, and `ignoreHeldEnter` moved from `clock/clock-focus.ts` to `@ui/focus-after` for every caller. (2) With codes on, Enter held down on the circle or "יציאה מהמשמרת" opened the camera and then pressed its "הפסקת הסריקה", which took the focus: ScanCircle's stop takes no key repeating either. (4, 11) After "כניסה בלי קוד" the confirm, disabled while it waited, had left the focus on the page's body, so the wait for a sheet still sliding away (`inClosingSheet` on the focus) never held, and the circle could take the focus while Radix still hid the page: ClockPanel now notes the sheet itself as it closes (`sheetLeaving`, set as `sheet` goes to null, cleared by `sheetGaveBack`) and waits for it wherever the focus is. (5) A refused approval of the last waiting entry (cancelled meanwhile, so the list read again took the card along) said nothing: ConfirmCard draws the alert where the card was when the card is gone. (6) A refusal in "כניסה בלי קוד" put its alert up while Radix moved the focus to the sheet's frame: WithoutCodeSheet holds it until nothing is on its way, puts the focus back on the confirm, and then says it. The digits' sheet when typing gets blocked (its slots go, the frame takes the focus) is as it was. (7) A scan refused for something other than the code (already in, no access…) said its alert as the camera gave the circle back: it is held as a press's refusal is, and said once the focus has landed. (8) The lock station's refused stop: held until the stop is over, the focus back on the stop (or on the lock the screen read again opened, for "gone"), then said, and drawn under the lock too. (9) A scanned punch said "נרשמה כניסה" twice, the camera's line and then the toast: the toast shows it and stays quiet (`quiet`). (10) The camera's two-minute give-up put the focus on the circle wherever the person had gone: only where it was lost now (`focusFirst`). Twin: `/design/shell/clock/code?other=prep&stop=failed` and `&stop=gone` (the stop refused, nothing stored). Tests (12, 13 among them): unit `focus-after` (`ignoreHeldEnter`, moved), `scan-circle` (its stop's key guard); e2e `design.spec.ts` (Enter held down from the lock twin's stop: "ביטול" focused, nothing restarted; the refused stops, the focus back, then the alert), `clock-code.spec.ts` (both sheets' slide stretched to two seconds, so the page read again always lands while the page is hidden; "כניסה בלי קוד" with no signal, the focus on the confirm before the alert; Enter held down on the circle with codes on, the camera still open; a scan refused as already in, the circle focused before the alert; the scanned punches' focus and quiet toasts, out from a break by "יציאה מהמשמרת"; the camera turning itself off after two minutes, on a fake clock, with the focus kept on the history it was moved to; the last approval refused as cancelled meanwhile, said after the page's title has the focus), `clock.spec.ts` (a refused press, the circle focused before the alert). Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright, `pnpm build`, VoiceOver in Safari.
- After the third check (eight findings, all kept, two of them one): (1, 8) ConfirmCard's refusal went only with the next "אישור", so the alert drawn where the card was stayed on `/clock/team`, and a list read again later (`RefreshOnReturn`, another manager's approval) drew it again out of context, where the card went or over another person's entry: the refusal keeps the list it came back with and shows only while that list is the one drawn (`refusalNow` in `clock/team/next-approval.ts`). (2) A scanned punch's quiet toast leaned on the camera's "נקלט. נרשמה כניסה", which the focus moving to the circle 1.2 seconds later could cut off: the toast is said again (ClockPanel's `quiet` is gone), and the camera's line keeps a code read to the eye (ScanCircle's new `quietRead`: `aria-live="off"` in the `read` state only), so it is said once, after the focus. (3) UndoRow's `autoFocus` "ביטול" took Enter still held down from the press that removed the row, and undid it (Today's bank change and first steps): its button takes `ignoreHeldEnter`. (4) "חזרה לסריקה" from either sheet put the focus on the camera's stop (`autoFocus`) while the closing sheet still hid the page, and it ended on the body once the sheet had gone: the camera brought back from a sheet doesn't focus its stop (ScanPunch's `focusStop`, from `scanFromSheet`), and the sheet gives it the focus once it has gone (ScanCircle's `cancel.ref`, the stop now last in `mainControl`). (5) "כניסה בלי קוד" refused: the alert waits for the page read again, and ✕, Escape, the scrim or a swipe in between closed the sheet with the refusal never said: the sheet keeps its close until it has said it (`closeHeldForRefusal` in `clock/clock-focus.ts`), and Sheet's `onOpenChange` doc names the exception. (6) The camera's two-minute give-up scrolled the page back up to the circle for someone who had only scrolled away: `preventScroll`. (7) Enter held down on "לא מצליחים? נרשמים בלי קוד" opened the sheet, and its repeat pressed the ✕ Radix focuses as a sheet opens: Sheet's ✕ takes `ignoreHeldEnter`, for every sheet (the header's action never takes the focus as it opens, so it is as it was). The README and `ignoreHeldEnter`'s doc name UndoRow's "ביטול" and the Sheet's ✕; `/design` draws the read ScanCircle with `quietRead`. Defaults applied, to ask Orel: a scan's confirmation is the toast, said after the focus, and the camera's line only shows it (not the other way round, nor both); a refused "כניסה בלי קוד" holds its sheet until it says why, rather than closing and leaving the circle to say it. Tests: unit `next-approval` (`refusalNow`), `clock-focus` (`closeHeldForRefusal`), `scan-circle` (the stop handed over without `autoFocus`, the read line's `aria-live`), `undo-row` and `sheet` (their key guards); e2e `clock-code.spec.ts` (back to the camera with the sheet's slide stretched: the stop focused once it has gone, nothing focused while hidden; Enter held down on "נרשמים בלי קוד", the sheet still open with its ✕ focused; Escape again and again after a press refused as already in, until the sheet says why; the refused approval gone once the list is read again with another entry waiting; the scanned punch's toast said once, with the focus on the circle, and the camera's line not live; the give-up with the page scrolled to its end, kept there), `design.spec.ts` (Enter held down on the gallery's undo row's remove, nothing undone). Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright, `pnpm build`, VoiceOver in Safari, NVDA.

## 2026-11-24 · chat · accessibility, part 6: the team and invites

- On chat/a11y-team from origin/main 76bb20c (#248). The sweep's clock-team findings 23, 27 and 28, and from the plan the name fix's "ביטול" on a person's page (F22's row); the clock's own findings are another part. - F23, deleting an invite on `/team`: its "ביטול" takes the focus the pressed "מחיקת ההזמנה" had (UndoRow `autoFocus`: both shapes draw it, and only the one on screen can take it), and the eight seconds wait while it is being reached (`onWaitingChange` to the held write's `pause`/`resume`, through the context's `waits`). Undone, the focus goes back to that row's "מחיקת ההזמנה" in whichever shape is on screen (`data-invite-cancel`, `inviteCancelTarget` in `team/invite-focus.ts`). Its time up, the focus goes to the title of the invites' section, read from a hidden anchor beside the row ("הזמנות" on a phone and a tablet, "כל האנשים בעסק" on a desktop); on a phone or a tablet with no invite left, to the page's title, since those lists then put the team first and draw the invites' section anew (`invitesLeft`).
- F27, the invite form (`/team/invite`, and the console's add-a-person, which draws the same form): each answer is heard once, as sign-in's number is (`answerHeard`, `useQuietWhy` and `useAnswerCount` from `app/(auth)/step-results.ts`). The first field refused, the name before the number (`firstRefused` in `team/invite/invite-answer.ts`), takes a focus that was lost or anywhere else in the form (the busy press, the number Enter was pressed in: `refusedAt`), is read as invalid, and why is said after its read, politely, out of its description while it keeps the focus; sent from that very field, the focus stays and why is said at once; a focus the person put outside the form stays there, why is said at once and stays in the field's description. The role's refusal is its list's own alert, and every other refusal a line above the press, now drawn anew for each answer (keyed by the count), with the press taking back the focus it lost. The link ready puts the focus on its card's title, "ההזמנה של … מוכנה" (tabIndex -1), or, with the focus put outside the form meanwhile, the announcer says it; and "הזמנה של מישהו נוסף" on the fresh form's name. `ResendLink`: the new link's copy button takes the focus from the press that went, a refusal puts it back on the press, and the line is drawn anew. `CopyLink`'s button is described by the link's label ("העתקת הקישור", "קישור ההזמנה של …"), here and in the console.
- F28, departments from a tablet up (`/manage/departments`, and the console's): the department whose people are shown beside the list has `aria-current="true"` on its people button (`usePeopleBeside`, `PEOPLE_BESIDE` in `manage/department-moves.ts`), the tint as it was; a choice there puts the focus on the panel's title, "מי ב…" (PageSection `headingRef`), each time, the same department again too. "הזזה למעלה" and "הזזה למטה" are `aria-disabled` at the ends (`movesOf`) instead of disabled, so the focus stays on them as the department arrives, and a press there moves nothing. A department moved stays the one shown: with none chosen, moving the first one down used to show the next first one's people under the focused button. A phone keeps its sheet, and marks none current.
- The name fix on a person's page: the sheet closes onto the fix's "ביטול", which came while it was open (Sheet `returnFocus`, UndoRow `buttonRef`, `quiet` so it isn't said twice; `fix` now says whether it wrote, `Fixed`); anything else closes onto the row "שם". Its eight seconds are a `heldTimer` that waits while "ביטול" is being reached (`undoWaits`), each fix is a row of its own (`key`), and once it goes with the focus the focus is back on the row, which stays reachable, dimmed, while the change back is on its way (ListRow `disabled="focusable"`).

The row "שם" is kept by `keepNameRow`, as the role row is, so MemberNameRow compiles with the React Compiler again (it wrote to a ref that came through the context). A deleted invite's row waits only for its own hold: `useHeldWrite`'s `pause` and `resume` take what they wait for and do nothing for anything else (`cancels.waits(id, …)`), so the earlier row, going as the next invite is deleted, no longer starts the next one's hold again while its "ביטול" has the keyboard. The role's refusal is drawn anew for each answer inside its list's alert (`key={answer}`), so the same refusal twice is heard. `PEOPLE_BESIDE` is `(min-width: 48rem)`, Tailwind's `md` in the CSS's own unit, so with a larger default font size the choice, the focus move and `aria-current` follow the layout. Tests: `department-moves` reads `--breakpoint-md` from the CSS; e2e: the departments twin moves the first one down with none chosen and keeps its people shown; the invite twin's "123" is answered as no number (the field sends only a whole one), with `pastSameWords` before the same words again, and the role refused twice is drawn anew with the press keeping the focus; the team twin's next deleted invite waits past the hold with the keyboard on its "ביטול" while the mouse rested over the earlier row; the person twin's name sheet stays through Escape and ✕ until the answer, then closes onto "ביטול". Left as it was: with the role refused together with the name, its alert still appears as the name takes the focus; whether that cuts the field's read short depends on the screen reader, and the review called it speculative.
- After the check (one finding): `team-invite.spec.ts`'s empty form looked for the name's "כתבו שם." on the whole page, and now that it is said, the announcer (outside `main`, its line kept seven seconds) holds it too, so the strict look found two and the real invite test would fail at every size. - After the second review (thirteen findings, two of them the same one; each fixed, none left as not real): a new link that arrives with the focus put elsewhere while its press was busy leaves the focus there and the announcer says "הקישור החדש של … מוכן" (new words `app.team.link.ready`), as the invite form says its link ready. A new link is kept for every shape at once (`InviteLinks` in `team/resend-link.tsx`, around the Team screen's body), so a screen that crosses the computer's width (a tablet turned) shows the same link in the other shape, never "קישור חדש" again. Undoing a deleted invite brings the restored "מחיקת ההזמנה" into view, centred, as it takes the focus out of view (`inViewWhenFocused` in `team/invite-focus.ts`), still nothing in `packages/ui`. The name sheet's own refusal (no name, too long) is no alert: it is said once through the announcer as the invite form's name is (`answerHeard`, `focusAt`, `useQuietWhy`), each refusal counted so the same one again is heard too. Departments: the failure's card is drawn anew for each failure (keyed by its stamp) and goes once a save goes through; Enter saves a name with the focus kept in its field (keyed by the department, its stored name put back after a failed save); "+" is `aria-disabled` with no name, so an add keeps the focus on it; the removal's "ביטול" gives the focus to the name it brought back (`departmentName`, `data-department-name`), else the list's title. Tests: unit `invite-focus` (`inViewWhenFocused`) and `department-moves` (`departmentName`); e2e on the twins: the undone invite's button in view after "ביטול" was scrolled to the top, each copy said and a refused one shown with the link selected, a new link ready with the focus elsewhere said, a refused one (the twin's new `?resend=refused`) drawn anew with the focus back on its press, the same link in the lists after the desktop's window is made narrower (desktop only), the invite form's link ready with the focus outside the form said and the next one's title focused, the name fix's quiet "ביטול" (nothing said by the announcer), a keyboard-only name fix whose "ביטול" waits past its eight seconds, the name sheet's refusal said once and again, the departments' failed saves drawn anew and cleared, the focus after Enter, "+" and "ביטול", and the department already shown chosen again; the real invite form's second "already in the team" drawn anew; the real and the demo name checks now look for the announcer's line, not an alert. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`; Playwright not run here.
