## 2026-11-23 · chat · the file's watch after a sleep, and its e2e race

- On chat/file-watch-sleep from origin/main 1a03386, rebased on origin/main 6ee0663 (H6b, #227, merged meanwhile). #229's phone run failed once in `employee-file-holders.spec.ts` ("a locked file asks for a code again", line 279: the file's heading gone after the page's own 15 minutes). The file's own check as it opens doesn't hide the file, so the test saw the file and jumped the page's clock while that check could still be unanswered; Playwright's `fastForward` fires each timer due within the jump once, at its end, so the same jump ran the check's 10 seconds' wait out, which closed the file (`failed`) for good on that screen.
- The test, first commit (54aa8ad): `hearFileCheck` (new `e2e/file-watch.ts`) listens on the file's channel before the file opens and waits for the opening check's word that a file is in use, which the check tells the device's other tabs just after clearing its wait (the response on the network comes before the page has handled it, so it can't say this), polled for as long as the page's own wait (`KEEP_OPEN_WAIT_MS`). Three tests jump past 10 seconds with a file open, and each waits for it now: that one, the accountant's minute for the account (same spec) and the twin's minute (`design.spec.ts`, on the twin's channel). On the rebase, H6b's `openFile` already waited for the same word in its own way, for every test in the spec; it now waits through `hearFileCheck`, so the spec's two tests need nothing of their own and there is one way. The other jumps (`employee-file.spec.ts`'s and the own-ID twin's minute, `clock-code.spec.ts`'s 21 seconds) have no watched file. Every assertion kept.
- The code, second commit: the same race for a real person. A check sent (the file opening, a press, coming back), then the phone locked or the device asleep before its answer was heard, and back more than 10 seconds later within the server's 15 minutes: the overdue give-up ran and closed the file in that tab, asking for a new code, though a reload would show it open (against rule point 3). Now a give-up that runs, or an answer that comes, more than `SLEPT_AFTER_MS` (the wait and 5 seconds for a busy page) after its check came after a sleep and decides nothing: with the page in sight and no look since, the server is asked again (`lookAgain(false)`: read only, unless a fresh press waits for its minute's end), the file hidden meanwhile; a look since asks for itself, and a page put away asks as it comes back; nothing more from that check is heard (not its time left, which counted from a late answer would outlast the server's, not its word to the other tabs, not its close). A check unanswered for 10 seconds in a tab awake still closes the file there. Rule points 3 to 6 in `open-watch.ts`, `KEEP_OPEN_WAIT_MS` and `check` say so.
- Tests: unit (5, a new block in `open-watch.test.ts`): asleep with the page in sight, just past the bound and for 5 minutes, nothing closes and the server is asked once, only to read, the file then shown for what the step-up has left and closed as the server's 15 minutes from the press end; a phone locked meanwhile asks once, as it comes back, whichever runs first; the old answer (open, closed or failed), before its give-up, before the new answer or after it, changes nothing; a look slept through that meets another tab's word first on waking still shows the file; awake, on time or up to 5 seconds late, the give-up still closes. Without the change the four slept tests fail; deferring to any newer check instead of a newer look fails the word's; ignoring only answers that come after a give-up fails the "before its give-up" case.
- Checked here: `pnpm check` before each commit. Not run here: Playwright, `pnpm build`, the database: CI is their first run.
- Defaults applied: the check asked again after a sleep keeps a fresh press's slide (point 4: one kept for the minute's end is never dropped); a page still put away asks nothing on waking (a hidden tab's timers can run late without a sleep, which would ask again and again of a server that hangs, and coming back asks anyway), so a file put away whose check went unanswered stays hidden in the page, not closed, until the page comes back or another tab locks the files. ROADMAP unchanged: nothing stays open.
- Next: CI's Playwright and build for this branch (H6b, the next planned, merged meanwhile).

## 2026-11-23 · chat · sign-in tells no one who is registered, a check counts once a code went, and a day's cap (security sweep)

- On chat/sec-auth from origin/main 27b229f, rebased on 675d460 (bigger teams, #230). Three findings of the security sweep before a first real business: the sign-in screen said whether a number was registered ("not registered yet", and the auth server's "too soon", which only a registered number meets); anyone could fill a number's checks with no code ever sent to it, silently locking it out of sign-in, the file's step-up and the bank code, again and again; and there was no cap past the quarter of an hour (some 960 guesses a day at a number's code).
- Sign-in: `sendSignInCode` in `app/(auth)/otp.ts`, used by `signIn` only, answers a number with no account and the auth server's short wait as a code sent; the send stays counted, and on sign-in the short wait's try is no longer given back, since only a registered number gets it and giving it back would let "too many" come sooner to an unknown one. `unknownPhone` is gone from `SignInError` and both message files, so no path can say "not registered". This reverses the decision of 15 October (run 5, chunk 4) that an unknown number gets "not registered yet". `sendCode` keeps its answers for joining and the two file codes, whose numbers have an account; one with none there is `failed`, logged without the number. Both share `requestCode`.
- The code step's words are true either way: "אם המספר … נוסף לצוות של עסק, שלחנו אליו קוד בן 6 ספרות", "ביקשנו קוד חדש למספר …", and a help always shown under the row, "לא הגיע קוד?" with the manager hint (`auth.signIn.noCode`, a section labelled by its h2). `tooMany` says "מאוחר יותר" on sign-in, the file's code and the bank code, since a day's window makes "a quarter of an hour" untrue. The same in the three shapes `AuthScreen` gives. Twin: `/design/screens/sign-in?step=code` (`SignInForm`'s new `start`).
- Migration `20261127000200_sign_in_windows.sql`: `private.auth_limits` is keyed by (kind, scope, per), with two day rows: 15 codes sent and 30 checked per number. `public.note_auth_attempt` is sealed.sql's with only these changes: every window of a kind and scope is read, the longest wait wins; and a check is counted for its number only while a send for it was counted in the last ten minutes (a code's lifetime in Twilio Verify), otherwise for its caller only, answering -1. `@db/auth-limits` exports `NO_CODE_WAITING` and takes -1; `checked()` turns it into `wrongCode` without asking the auth server, for sign-in, joining, the bank code and the holder's step-up. Same signature, so `database.types.ts` is unchanged; the seed's update lifts the new rows too. Before it reaches a project the app sees no -1 and one window per scope, as before.
- Tests: unit `otp.test.ts` (both sends, both checks with no code waiting), `actions.test.ts` (signing in), new `packages/db/auth-limits.test.ts` and `app/(auth)/sign-in/sign-in-form.test.ts` (the code step drawn the same for any number in each state, the help in both languages, no `unknownPhone`, no exclamation marks); pgTAP `auth_limits.test.sql` (the day rows deleted inside it, a send before the checks, 20) and new `auth_limits_windows.test.sql` (35, with the review's below); e2e `askForCode` asks `dev.sms_codes` by id whether a code came (`lastCodeId`, `codeAfter`), since the screen no longer says "too soon", and `sign-in.spec.ts` replaces the unknown-number and too-soon tests (word for word the same code step, no code to the unknown number; a resend at once sends nothing and the first code still signs in) and adds a check with no code sent (`forgetSentCodes`, `noteSentCode`); `screens.spec.ts` lists the twin.
- Docs: ROADMAP row 49, known issue 67, new known issues 94 to 98 (81 to 85 are bigger teams', 86 to 93 held by the open hiring file, production and copy-week pull requests), `supabase/README.md` (the windows, the ten minutes, clearing a locked number), `packages/db/README.md`, and CLAUDE.md's sign-in line.
- After the review: a number with no account yet could still be filled with nobody seeing it, since sign-in counts what is sent to it and sends nothing, and with the day's windows some 15 sends and 30 checks a day would hold an invited person out of `/join` (whose code counts against the same number) for a day. Joining now clears such a number's counts before its code is counted: `sendJoinCode` in `app/(auth)/otp.ts`, `forgetNumberAttempts` in `@db/auth-limits`, and `public.forget_number_attempts` in the same migration (service role only, `database.types.ts` by hand). The database clears nothing for a number with an account, however `auth.users` spells it, and keeps the callers' counts; joining makes the account before its code goes, so a number is cleared until it has one and never after. Tests: unit `otp.test.ts` (cleared before the count, in one spelling; a failed clear still counts and sends) and `auth-limits.test.ts`; pgTAP `auth_limits_windows.test.sql` (a stranger fills a new number's day, joining clears it, the stranger's own counts stay, numbers with an account keep theirs, only the server clears). Known issue 95 and the migration's header say so. The rebase moved the five new known issues to 94 to 98, after the numbers the open pull requests hold.
- After the second review: (1) sign-in kept a send the auth server refused for its project-wide SMS cap or its limit per address (both read as its short wait), so while the cap was used up a stranger could fill a registered number's day with no code reaching it, and people used up their own day pressing "send a new code". The app now has its own wait between two codes to one number, the same for every number: `private.auth_send_wait` (one row, 65 seconds: the auth server's 60 by default and five to spare, since it marks a code sent a moment after the app counts it), read in `public.note_auth_attempt` from the number's last counted send, under the number's lock so two at once meet it too. A send inside it is counted for its caller only and answers -2 (`CODE_JUST_SENT` in `@db/auth-limits`), and the auth server isn't asked: sign-in answers it as a code sent, joining and the file's codes say "in a few seconds" (`rateLimit`). Past it, whatever the auth server still refuses (its SMS cap, its limit per address) and a failing SMS provider are given back for the number and the caller (`forgetAuthAttempt`, now in `requestCode` for every flow) and answered `failed`, on sign-in, joining, the file's step-up and the bank code alike. A number with no account still reaches the code step while the cap is used up or the provider is down: known issue 98, widened; 96 says 65 seconds; ROADMAP row 49 names the wait. The seed sets it to 2.25 seconds, the local auth server's 2 and a quarter, under the 2.5 the e2e wait out. (2) `/join` said too many and too soon on the invite step, so whoever held a live invite could tell whether the invited number already had an account (its counts kept, where a new number's are cleared). On the first send both are now the code step, word for word as a code that went, and that step's words are true either way: "קוד בן 6 ספרות מגיע בהודעת SMS למספר שמסתיים ב-… אם לא הגיע, אפשר לבקש קוד חדש בעוד דקה." A malformed number and a failure stay on the invite step. A new code asked for says each as it is, and known issue 94 now names what that leaves: after five sign-in codes to the number, the second press tells, and a person with an account sees the five codes. Tests: unit `otp.test.ts` (a second send inside the wait asks the auth server nothing and gives nothing back; past it `over_sms_send_rate_limit`, `over_request_rate_limit` and a provider failure give the try back and fail, on sign-in and in `sendCode`; a failed give-back still fails), `auth-limits.test.ts` (-2) and new `app/(auth)/join/actions.test.ts`; pgTAP `auth_limits_windows.test.sql` (48: one row; a second send within the wait answers -2, counted for its caller only, for a number with an account and one without; it doesn't lengthen the wait; a send given back starts none; the caller's limit still holds inside it; nobody else reads the row; the two 30-second gaps there became 90 under the test's 65-second wait) and `auth_limits.test.sql` (the wait at a second, under its gaps); e2e `employee-file.spec.ts`'s second join asks `dev.sms_codes` whether a code came and otherwise asks for a new one, as `askForCode` does, and `sign-in.spec.ts`'s "at once" test restarts the app's wait (`noteSentCode`) instead of the auth server's (`restartSmsWait`, removed), since past the app's wait the auth server's would now be a failure. Docs: the migration's header, `supabase/README.md` (the wait, the give-back, `/join`'s first answer, and a dashboard line to keep the auth server's wait a few seconds shorter than the app's), `packages/db/README.md`, and the comments in config.toml and e2e/session.ts. Until the migration reaches a project nothing holds a code inside the auth server's wait, which sign-in then answers as a failure for a registered number only: the Migrate workflow runs when this merges. Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: the migration, pgTAP, Playwright. Defaults applied (to ask Orel): the app's own wait is 65 seconds on a hosted project and 2.25 locally; the auth server's limits on everyone and a failing provider read "משהו השתבש" on every flow, with the try given back; on `/join`'s first send too many and too soon read as a code that went, with the new words, and "יותר מדי ניסיונות" shows from the second press.
- After the third review: (1) `/join` still told the link's holder from its second answer whether the invited number had an account, with only one sign-in code: the app's wait runs from the number's last counted send, which a number with an account keeps from that sign-in code and one with none had cleared, so a new code asked for at the right moment read "in a few seconds" for one and went for the other. A new code asked for too soon on `/join` now reads as on sign-in, a code asked for (`resent`), and `auth.join.codeResent` says "ביקשנו קוד חדש למספר שמסתיים ב-… אם לא הגיע, אפשר לבקש שוב בעוד דקה.", true whether a code went or not. Known issue 94 and `supabase/README.md` now name what is left, all through "too many": five sign-in codes then a new code on `/join`, or one sign-in code and ten wrong checks then a check on `/join`, where the person with an account sees only one stray code; 96 says joining reads as sign-in. Test: `join/actions.test.ts` (too soon on a new code equals one that went). (2) The app's wait read only sends stamped up to the one being counted, but the app stamps a send before it reaches the database, so of two sent at once the one stamped later could take the number's lock first and the other passed the wait and both went. The wait now holds on both sides of a counted send (`a.at <= at_time + send_wait` in `public.note_auth_attempt`, the migration changed in place). Test: pgTAP `auth_limits_windows.test.sql` (51: a send stamped a second before one already counted answers -2 and is not counted for the number); every earlier expected value is the same under the new bound.
- Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: the migration, pgTAP, `pnpm build`, Playwright (CI first).
- Defaults applied (to ask Orel), as recommended: 15 sends and 30 checks a day per number, beside 5 and 10 a quarter of an hour; no day's cap per address, since a business's Wi-Fi and the carriers share one; a check counts for a number for ten minutes after a send; "לא הגיע קוד?" always shown at the foot of the code step; "אפשר לנסות שוב מאוחר יותר" for too many; a new code asked for too soon on sign-in reads "ביקשנו קוד חדש" as for any number, and the earlier code still works; a number locked for the day is cleared by the team with SQL; no lock after many wrong codes in a row for now.

## 2026-11-23 · chat · a sheet closes right after a refusal

- On chat/sheet-close-refusal from origin/main 272ae2e. CI failed once on desktop at `e2e/clock-team.spec.ts` "a manager adds a shift someone forgot": ✕ pressed right after the overlap refusal left the sheet open. The cause was in the app: the sheet's close was held on `useTransition`'s pending, which a server action that calls `refresh()` after a refusal (the clock team's add, correct, cancel, bring back and break edits, `asManager`, `afterBreakEdit`) keeps true until the page read again is streamed and drawn. Next resolves the action's answer first (server-action-reducer.js, `resolve(actionResult)`, then the navigation), the refusal is set after the await, outside the transition, and shows at once, and the close pressed meanwhile was dropped with nothing asking again. A manager on a slow phone saw the error, pressed ✕, and nothing happened until the page came back.
- Fix: `useSheetWrite` (`packages/ui/use-sheet-write.ts`): `pending` is the transition's, for the spinner and the disabled buttons, so no second write goes out against old data; `held()` lasts from the press until the write's answer (a count, released in `finally`, so a throw lets go too), and the sheet's `onOpenChange` reads it. Used by `/clock/team/[person]` (every write from its sheet; the toast's "ביטול" keeps its own transition) and the clock's sheet without a code (`clock-panel.tsx`, whose punch refreshes after a refusal too). `Sheet`'s `onOpenChange` says so beside it.
- Checked and left: the pay sheets (`close-month.tsx`'s reopening, `wage-card.tsx`, `details-card.tsx`'s two) hold on their transitions, but their actions refresh only on success, where the code closes the sheet itself; the punch code sheet, the wording editor, the station lock, the list's three sheets, the availability sheets, the builder's shift sheet and its leave sheet (hidden while a publish is held, never refusing a close), the business switcher and every other `Sheet` pass a close through unheld.
- Tests: unit `use-sheet-write.test.ts` (held from the press to the answer while a refreshing transition is still pending; let go on a throw, which goes on; held until the last of two; the hook drawn). e2e: both places in `clock-team.spec.ts` (the add's overlap, and bringing a cancelled shift back over the right one) arm a `MutationObserver` that presses ✕ in the same turn the refusal is drawn, before the refreshed page can be, then expect the sheet gone and read what the refusal said; the second opens the shift again for the axe scan of its refusal, as before. Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: `pnpm build` and Playwright: CI is their first run.
- After the check: on the clock screen nothing read the sheet's `pending` once the sheet without a code could close at its answer, so after a refusal (`notIn` after a clock-out on another device, say) the circle, "Start break", "End shift" and the clock-out undo could send a write against the old page before the one read again was drawn. `clock-panel.tsx` now gives them `busy`, both transitions (`pending || noneBusy`); the sheet still closes at the answer. Unit `clock-panel.test.ts` draws the panel in its four states with the press without a code on its way: every one of its own buttons waits, and each can be pressed with nothing on its way (failed before the fix). Left as it was before this branch: the code sheet and the camera, whose writes run in their own transitions the panel doesn't see, and which never held anything.
- Next: nothing left of it. Defaults applied: none.

## 2026-11-23 · chat · CI's e2e in six parts

- On chat/e2e-shards from origin/main 27b229f. Promised to Orel on 22 November: CI took about 50 minutes, most of it the desktop job's 38 to 45 (1083 tests on one worker). Each screen size now runs in two jobs side by side (`--shard=1/2`, `2/2`), six in all, each on its own runner with its own local Supabase, so no job shares the demo data with another. With `fullyParallel` off Playwright gives each part whole files, in order. Expected: about 25 to 30 minutes a run, on GitHub's free minutes for a public repository.
- The jobs are named `e2e (phone 1/2)` and so on, their results `playwright-results-<size>-<part>` and their records `passed-e2e-<size>-<part>-<tree>`; `.github/scripts/tested-tree.sh` now asks for all eight records (check, database and the six) before main skips a run. A pull request run from before this change holds the old names, so a merge of such a tree runs everything on main once. README's CI paragraph says so.
- Risk: a file that relied on an earlier file's leftovers in the same job now starts on a fresh database; CI's first run shows it. Checked here: nothing (YAML and the script only); CI is the test.

## 2026-11-23 · chat · copying last week on the schedule builder

- On chat/copy-week from origin/main 27b229f. Orel's 22 November order (today's features ready for an existing business) and the approved improvement "העתקה משבוע שעבר" (ROADMAP "מה הוחלט", docs/AGENT.md's backlog). Filling the week from availability stays for later.
- No migration: the copy only changes the screen. The builder already keeps the week in client state until a dock button sends it, and a draft save (`public.save_week_schedule`) writes no audit row and sends no push, so a copy saved is a draft and nobody hears of it until it is published. Every rule of the week's write (active members, positions, two shifts at once in the week and across its edges, the basis) still applies at save time.
- Domain: `packages/domain/copy-week.ts`. `copySourceOf` (last week once built, its shifts without ids, positions the business still has), `copyLastWeek` (by weekday; never into the past, as `draftAhead`; a day last week had nothing on stays; a day with people on a shift still to start stays unless the switch replaces it; otherwise its own shifts still to start make way; who left counted, who is off the staff, whose answer for this week doesn't cover the shift and who would work two shifts at once, last week's night into this week's first day included, left out with the reason, the shift copied with its place open; the week's limit), `copiedOver`. 24 unit tests, every rule numbered, with a week from Monday, no positions, no pattern, a night on the last day and the result parsed by `weekSchedule`.
- Server: `currentCopySource(start)` in `app/(app)/schedule/week.ts` (cached; next week reuses this week's `currentScheduleBuild`, the running week reads the week before through `getWeek` and `getWeekSchedule` under RLS; a failed read logs a message only and offers no copy). `saveScheduleAction` takes the hidden `copied` field, the save moment the screen's reading had, and refuses a save or publish of a copy into a week saved since (`copySaved`) before anything is written.
- Screen: "העתקה מהשבוע שעבר", a secondary pill with `CopyPlus` at the end of the status row, on a draft only (not on a published week, one only read or one over, nor without a last week), disabled while the dock is busy. `copy-sheet.tsx`: the dates of last week, a line when it is a draft only saved, how many shifts and people, each day and what becomes of it, the switch "להחליף גם ימים שכבר יש בהם אנשים" while a day has people, who wasn't copied by reason (who left counted, never named), the line that nothing is saved or sent, and the main button; or why there is nothing to copy. Applied: the builder's one toast says so with "ביטול" for 10 seconds, which puts back the screen as it was. `SheetSection` moved to `sheet-section.tsx` (with an h4 level) for both sheets. The empty week's card goes once the screen has shifts.
- Words: `app.build.copy.*` and `app.build.errors.copySaved`, he and en, no business kind named.
- Twins: the running twin's next week (`?week=next`) copies the shop's week under way (Liya, who left, counted; Tamar, mornings only next week, left out of Thursday evening), and `&state=draft` has Tamar and Itai on Sunday, kept unless replaced. The restaurant twin has no last week, as the demo restaurant's weeks start empty.
- Tests: e2e `schedule-build.spec.ts` four new (copy, see who was left out, undo, save as a draft with nothing published or logged; a day with people kept unless replaced; offered only on a draft with a last week; a copy into a week saved meanwhile refused), with `buildDemoThisWeek`, `demoWeekPublished` and `scheduleAssignments(week)` in `e2e/db.ts`; `design.spec.ts` the new axe page, a new twin test and no copy on the other builder twins.
- Docs: ROADMAP's row, "אחר כך", "מה הוחלט", known issue 8 and the new 93 (the week after the running week isn't checked in the sheet, and two copies of empty shifts saved in the same instant could both go in), docs/AGENT.md's backlog lines, the domain README.
- Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright and the dev server (none here): CI is their first run, so the screens weren't seen at 390, 820 and 1440 here.
- Defaults applied (to ask Orel), as recommended: a day with only empty shifts takes last week's in their place; a day with people stays, with the switch; a last week only saved is copied and said to be a draft; no last week, no button; someone whose availability doesn't fit is left out with the shift copied; someone who sent nothing is copied; the running week as a draft copies only what is still to start; nothing is saved until saving or publishing, with "ביטול" for 10 seconds; the shifts that make way leave the screen, back only through "ביטול"; no setting per business; Shabbat times as the week's note shows them, hours unchanged; two managers checked in the server, no migration; the demo restaurant stays without a schedule.
- After review (each finding confirmed by a skeptic): the copy's "ביטול" put back the whole screen as it was before the copy, so a change made within its 10 seconds (a person added on a day the copy left alone, say) went with it, with nothing to bring it back. Now the first change on the screen after a copy (a shift added, changed, removed or brought back: every path through `edited`) takes the toast away with its way back (`changedAfterCopy` in `ScheduleBuilder`, the builder's `onEdited`), and the copy stays as any other work on the screen; a second copy's toast takes the first one's place. A day whose copied shifts would all have begun said "כבר עבר" even while today's were under way: it now says "המשמרות שהיו מועתקות אליו כבר התחילו, ולכן הוא נשאר כמו שהוא", and the sheet's line when nothing is left "אין מה להעתיק: כל המשמרות שהיו מועתקות כבר התחילו השבוע." Tests: the twin's e2e changes Sunday evening after the draft's copy and finds the toast gone, the copy and the change kept. ## 2026-11-23 · chat · security before a real business: files through the server only

- On chat/sec-files, stacked on chat/scale-fixes (#230, origin/main 2c50747); rebased onto main once #230 merges. Orel's order of 22 November on #1: getting ready for a real business. Three of the security sweep's findings: chat files, list photos and profile photos still had a read policy on `storage.objects`, so a session's own token, copied from the browser, could sign a link of any lifetime (working after offboarding and after a message's removal, served without the app's row check or /chat-files' rules); the avatars policy took a person's whole folder, so a replaced or removed photo whose removal failed stayed readable; and one person could push out every chat file of the business, those of chats they don't read among them.
- Migration `20261127000300_files_server_only.sql` (000200 left free on purpose): drops "people read the files of the chat messages they read", then `private.can_read_chat_file`; "people read the photos of the lists they see", then `private.can_read_list_photo`; "people and their colleagues read photos", and revokes `private.avatar_owner` from authenticated. No bucket has a policy now. New: `public.sees_profile_photo(photo_path)` (the reader's session: true only while the folder's profile points at the photo, for the person and their active colleagues); `public.set_avatar_path(person, new_path)` (the secret key's: the pointer swapped under the row's lock, the photo it replaced given back; P0002, 23514, 55000); `public.expired_avatars(at_time, max_rows)` (the secret key's: avatar files a day old that no profile points at, in the app's shape, oldest first). Replaced, each its newest body plus only the change: `private.list_photos_tick` (20261110001500_list_photos.sql's; also while an avatar is due), `public.expired_files` (20261111000700_documents.sql's; `live` is now ordered within each sender's half first, so past the space what one person holds beyond half of it goes first, their oldest first; under the space nothing changes, and one sender keeps today's order) and `public.reserve_chat_upload` (20261111000600_chat_pdfs.sql's; one check under the business's lock, between 'pdfs' and 'busy': the sender's own PDFs of the 10 minutes, with this one's 10 MiB, bring at most half the space, 54000 with hint 'own'). Three entries in database.types.ts by hand.
- Server: `chatFileBytes` and `chatPdfDownload` read the message under the session first, then the file with the secret key (no session download, no `Promise.all`); `listPhotoBytes` reads the item under RLS (its path, its photo still kept), then the secret key; `profilePhotoBytes` asks `sees_profile_photo`, then the secret key; `setProfilePhoto`/`removeProfilePhoto` go through `set_avatar_path` and remove what it gave back (tried twice, logged by code; an answer lost keeps the upload for the sweep); `cleanProfilePhotos` runs after `cleanListPhotos` in `/cron/list-photos`, each in its own try. `profilePhotoUrls` and `PROFILE_PHOTO_URL_SECONDS` are gone; `profilePhotoOwner` and `CHAT_SENDER_SPACE_DIVISOR` are new. `storedHead` keeps its 60-second link, the server's own.
- Words: `app.chat.pdfFailure.busyOwn` ("שלחת הרבה קבצי PDF בדקות האחרונות. אפשר לנסות שוב בעוד כמה דקות", `busyOwn` → retry, as `busy`), and `modules.chat.settings.fileSpaceMb.hint` says one person's oldest go first past half. The 15 minutes are the app's server's, not the database's (ROADMAP 76).

The idle lock fails closed and keeps its moment (`./team/[person]/file/open-watch.ts`, `watchOpenFile`): a keep-open that fails (offline, or the action's error) or answers closed closes the file; the last activity is kept in a ref while Next hides the screen in an <Activity> and shows it again, and the watch starts in a layout effect, so a screen shown again past its 15 minutes closes before it is painted, asking nothing. `GatedFile` (`file-gate.tsx`) draws the locked card in the file's place at once, without waiting for the server or its network, the same card at the same place that the server's next drawing lands on, so a code asked for there stays asked for; then the files are locked and the page drawn again (`lockAndDraw`, which draws even when the lock fails), and "נעילת התיק" closes the same way. Default applied (to ask Orel): a file whose keep-open doesn't reach the server locks, so a phone that loses its network for a moment asks for a code again.
- After the second review (four findings, each confirmed): a lock is heard in every tab. `./team/[person]/file/file-tabs.ts` (`deviceFileTabs`) talks to the device's other tabs on the BroadcastChannel `conceptapp-file-open`, or through a storage event under the same key where there is none, never hearing itself and carrying only "closed" or the moment of the last activity; the twin has a channel of its own, so a mock file's lock never closes a real one. `lockAndDraw` tells the other tabs at once, before the server answers, when the files lock here ("נעילת התיק", idle, no answer), never for a close the server answered (that may be another business's step-up now) and never back to the tab that told; a tab that hears it closes the file at once (`elsewhere`), locks too and draws itself again. A page put away hides the file (invisible and inert, `hold`), and coming back, or Next showing the screen again, asks the server at once, whatever the minute's throttle says, the file hidden until it answers open and the focus it held given back; only the last look's answer shows it. Better than proposed: activity in one tab is told to the others with each touch and starts their 15 minutes again, so the device locks after 15 minutes with none in any tab, as the server's step-up does, and a tab left open beside the one in use no longer locks it. Focus (`file-focus.ts`, `focusIfLost`, only when what held it went): "התיק נעול" (or the duties' title) takes it where an open file closed, whatever closed it (`GatedFile`'s `wasOpen`, not only a close here, so a number's "הצגה" that finds the step-up gone lands there too); after a right code the file's first heading ("פרטים מזהים", every section's `h2` now `tabIndex={-1}`); the code's line is a `role="status"`, saying "שלחנו קוד חדש" from the second code on (`code.resent`), its node new each time, so a resend is heard. A view as the accountant goes back to "צוות" (`FilesReader`'s `team` reader carries `seesTeam`), not to a profile that sends it to the file again. With no manager or worker yet, the accountant's Home card says "עוד אין עובדים בצוות. כשיצטרפו, התיקים שלהם יופיעו כאן." with no "complete" line and no "לתיקים", and the files-only `/team` an `EmptyState` (h2) in every shape instead of an empty list and a table of headers, under "עוד אין תיקים של הצוות". Tests: unit for the tabs (8, and `newsOf`), the watch (8 more: put away and back, a lock elsewhere seen on coming back, offline on coming back, the last look only, opened while put away, shown again by Next, another tab's lock and its activity) and `lockAndDraw` (2 more), `focusIfLost` (4), the focus's places and the status line, the view's way back, the empty card and the empty team; e2e: the focus after a code and after "נעילת התיק", and a file locked in one tab closing at once in another. Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright and `pnpm build`.
- After the check of the second review's fixes (five findings, two of them the same): the server decides each extension. Another tab's activity starts a tab's 15 minutes again and asks the server too, at most once a minute, telling nobody back, so a file whose step-up is gone (signed out in another window, or another person's or business's now) closes as the server says (`closed`: nothing told, nothing locked) instead of staying up while someone works in another window. A screen Next shows again is never closed by its own clock, which heard nothing while it was hidden: it is held before it is painted and the server asked, whatever its own last activity, shown on an open answer and closed as `closed` on a closed one, so pressing Back to an old file no longer locks the device's files in every tab. On an open answer to coming back or being shown again, the screen's 15 minutes start from the answer, as the server's did, and the other tabs are told, as of every slide, so a screen shown again at 14 minutes doesn't lock a minute later. An answer that doesn't come within 10 seconds (`KEEP_OPEN_WAIT_MS`) closes the file as one that failed would, and while the file is held for longer than 300 ms a quiet line with a spinner says "בודקים שהתיק עדיין פתוח" in its place (`app.team.file.checking`, a `role="status"` always there), so it never just vanishes. Signing out (`SignOutForm`, now `signOutAndLeave` in `app/sign-out.ts`, the station's sign-out too) and locking a device as a station (`LockStation`, not on /design's twins) close every open file on the device at once (`closeFilesOnDevice`), before the page goes. Tests: unit for the watch (6, in place of 2, and 3 changed: no answer, no answer on coming back, shown again at 14 minutes, shown again past its own 15 minutes answered open and closed, another tab's activity asking once a minute, and closed by the server on it), `closeFilesOnDevice` (2) and `signOutAndLeave` (4). Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright and `pnpm build`. Default applied (to ask Orel): signing out on /design's station twin also closes the device's real open files, since the form can't tell the twin's stand-in from a real sign-out.
- After the final check (three findings, each confirmed: an idle tab's failed check on another tab's activity locked the tab in use; another tab's word extended a tab's 15 minutes without asking, so a file whose step-up another business's had replaced stayed on screen; a tab back from a phone's freeze judged "idle" by a clock that had heard nothing, and locked the device), a simpler rule, numbered the same in `./team/[person]/file/open-watch.ts`. 1. Only the server says whether the files are open on this device: the step-up cookie, slid only by a check for the person's activity in a tab (point 5) and gone by itself 15 minutes after the last. 2. Only a deliberate act closes them on the whole device: "נעילת התיק" or signing out, which tell every tab `closed` and forget the cookie, as before. 3. Anything else closes the file in its own tab only, telling no tab and forgetting nothing: the server answering closed, a check that fails or gets no answer within 10 seconds, another tab's lock heard (which no longer locks again). A tab's own 15 minutes passing, a page coming back (past them too, after a freeze) and a screen Next shows again close nothing by themselves: the file is hidden and the server asked once; open shows it again (the person is busy in another tab), closed or failed closes it in that tab. `closeAndDraw` tells and locks for `pressed` only, in place of `lockAndDraw`, and the `idle` reason is gone. 4. A tab's 15 minutes end when the server's do: its own check, answered open, says how long the step-up has left (15 minutes once slid, never more), counted from the answer, which comes after the server's moment and the cookie's, so when they pass the server finds the step-up gone unless activity in some tab slid it since. A press, a key and another tab's word ask the server at most once a minute, one within the minute kept for its end rather than dropped; the word alone extends nothing. 5. Only a check for the person's activity in a tab (the file opening, a press or a key, coming back, shown again by Next) slides the step-up, and only it, answered open, tells the other tabs `active`; a check for the tab's own 15 minutes or for another tab's word only reads the step-up and tells nothing. So tabs left alone never keep each other open: the files close on the device 15 minutes after the last activity in any tab, a minute more at most for a press kept for the minute's end, which slides only while fresh (`PRESS_FRESH_MS`, the minute and 5 seconds): run long after in a tab frozen in the background, its check only reads (a check of that fix found it; a unit test fails without it). 6. While a file is held for an answer, "בודקים שהתיק עדיין פתוח" after 300 ms and the 10-second give-up, which closes that tab only. A message carries its kind alone now (`active` lost its moment; an older build's is left), and `OpenFileMemory` keeps only whether the screen showed the file. The focus and its announcements stay as in 556f129. Tests: the watch's unit tests rewritten on a device of tabs and a stand-in for the server's step-up, one per point and the three findings' cases (an idle tab's failed check never locks the tab in use, twice: a failed action and a lost answer; a replaced step-up is never extended by heard activity; a tab resumed after a freeze never locks the device, with its timer, its coming back or the words first), five of the screen's own, `closeAndDraw` (3), and the tabs' messages by kind; e2e: the idle test passes the page's 15 minutes first (the server says open, the file shows again, the cookie kept), then clears the cookie for the server's; the two-tab lock is unchanged. Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright and `pnpm build`. Default applied (to ask Orel): a tab that closes on its own while the device's step-up is still fresh (a check lost on a weak network) shows "התיק נעול" and asks for a code, though reloading it would show the file.
- After the check of those fixes (one finding, confirmed: every check slid the step-up, a tab's own 15 minutes and a word heard included, so two idle tabs whose checks came under 15 minutes apart kept each other's file open for good, with nobody at the device, and "הצגה" kept working for a passer-by): point 5 above, and point 4's time left. `keepFileOpenAction(business, slide)` takes the slide as a zod-parsed boolean, refusing anything else, under the same gate (`fileHolder`) and the same no-store answer of a POST: `true` slides as before (`keepFileOpen`) and answers 15 minutes left; `false` only reads (`fileOpenLeft` in `step-up.ts`, which `fileOpenFor` now uses, never sealing the cookie again) and answers the step-up's time left (`stepUpLeft` in `@domain/file-holders`: 15 minutes less its age, never more than 15). `KeepOpenAnswer` is `{ open: true, leftMs }` or `{ open: false }`; the watch slides only for its tells, sets its 15 minutes to the answer's moment plus the time left, and closes the file (`failed`) on a time left that isn't one; the twin answers 15 minutes. Tests: unit for `stepUpLeft`, the action (both ways under each gate, a read that slides nothing and says the time left, a slide that isn't a boolean refused), and the watch (points 1, 4 and 5 changed to the time left and to reads that slide nothing; tabs left alone: two idle tabs 30 seconds apart, hearing each other or not, both closed within 16 minutes of the last press with the step-up over 15 minutes old; a check for the clock or a word never slides; a lone tab still closes 15 minutes after its last press; the tab in use keeps itself and the idle tab beside it open; at most 15 minutes from any answer, and a time left that isn't one closes); the three findings' cases unchanged. The e2e idle test changes only in its comment: the page's 15 minutes ask only to read, the server says open, and the second 15 minutes, past the time it said, find the cookie cleared. Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright and `pnpm build`.

## 2026-11-22 · chat · a former worker reads their old file (profile H5c, #82)

- On chat/hiring-file-past from origin/main 0e3c858 (H4 #218, H5a #222 and H5b #223 merged). The design's §2.4 (the person reads their own file the same way, also after leaving, unlogged), §2.9, §3.1 item 8 ("עבודות קודמות") and §5's H5 row (demo: a worker who left reads their old file); the H5b plan's §14 and its default 13.

Two pgTAP assertions in `employee_files.test.sql` (someone who left reveals their own number, not logged; plan 77 → 79).
- The route: a new route group `app/(account)` (a signed-in person's own screens that need no business; H8's export and account deletion fit there too) with a signed-in-only layout, at `/me/past/<business>`, which no `(app)` route has and `proxy.ts` already guards. Outside the app's frame for everyone, someone in another business too, since the `(app)` layout draws the screen with no business in place of every page and isn't drawn again on a client navigation.
- The gate (`app/(account)/me/past/past-file.ts`), in order: the id a uuid (any case); someone signed in; the business among their departures (`currentDepartures`, now a per-request `cache` in `app/(app)/departures.ts`, which `currentDeparture` reads too); a view session refused before any file is read; then `getEmployeeFile` for the session's own person. Off says so, nothing of theirs says so, no keys says "not available yet", anything else is not found (the root's), the same for each. Each read in a try/catch with `unstable_rethrow` first, logging a code and a kind only. The reveal is a POST action (`revealPastAction`) through the same gate (`pastReader`).
- The screen: read-only, no main action and no write, in the business's colour, in Hebrew or in English if the profile chose English. Who they are (the ID or document masked with "הצגה", a passport's country and expiry, the names, the birth date), the address and the ways to reach them (titled as step 2 is), the account (bank and branch, whose, the number masked), the pension as answered, each year's Form 101, and "מה נשמר אצל {business}". A part a holder handed on says so; a part with nothing says "לא נמסר". Phone one column; tablet two (who they are and how to reach them | the rest); desktop the same, the first wider. Its other states (`off`, `empty`, `viewing`, `unavailable`) in the same frame with one way back.
- The ways in: `PastJobsSection` ("עבודות קודמות", "התיק שלי ב{business}", "בצוות עד {month}" in Israel's zone) on `/me` after "העסקים שלי", and on `NoBusinessScreen` (new `past` prop: the list, then the way out), only for a business with the module on and something of theirs in the file, each read once.
- The words promise what the code keeps: the details stay with the business after leaving, they can be seen here while the business uses the file, managers no longer see the emergency contact (`team_emergency_contacts` joins active members), and the business is the one to ask for a fix or a deletion. No length of time is said: nothing deletes a file yet (H8).

## 2026-11-22 · chat · sheets rise above the phone keyboard

- On chat/sheet-keyboard from origin/main 7d2f693. Orel's report of 22 November, on his phone: a small sheet with a field to type in opens the keyboard, and the keyboard covers the sheet. The cause: `Sheet` is `position: fixed` at the foot of the layout viewport, which iOS never shrinks for the keyboard and Android Chrome by default doesn't either (it shrinks only the visual viewport).
- `packages/ui/use-keyboard-lift.ts`: `keyboardLift` (pure) reads the two viewports, `layoutHeight - (visualViewport.height + offsetTop)`, never below 0, smaller as iOS moves the visual viewport down to a field, nothing under 40px, and nothing for a pinch (`scale` not 1); `watchKeyboard` follows the visual viewport's resize and scroll and the window's resize (turning the phone), says only changes, and once it has stood still 250ms with a keyboard up brings the field with the focus into sight within the sheet's body (`keepFocusInView`, `nearestScroll`: `block: "nearest"` by hand, since `scrollIntoView` would also move the page behind and the visual viewport); `useKeyboardLift` runs it while the sheet's scrim is drawn, the scrim's height being the layout viewport's (`innerHeight` follows the visual viewport on some browsers). No visualViewport, nothing lifted.
- `Sheet`: `--sheet-bottom` (8px over the keyboard) and `--sheet-max` (what is on screen, 8px from the top and its safe area) in its style, read by its classes with the old place as their fallback, so with no keyboard (a tablet, a desktop) nothing changes. `bottom` and `max-height` ease over 200ms, none with reduced motion; the drag and the way in and out keep the transform. Every sheet has it, ShareSheet, AttachSheet and the rest through `Sheet`. No `interactive-widget` in the viewport meta.
- Not changed: the chat's `Composer` is sticky in the conversation's own page, not fixed over a locked one, so the browser's own focus scroll takes it up as any field on a page, and no one has seen it covered; `FloatingDock` and the availability form's dock hold buttons, the tab bar links and the skeleton its placeholders, no field; `ChoiceMenu` is placed by Radix within the screen and has no field.
- Tests: unit (`use-keyboard-lift.test.ts`: iOS, Android, no keyboard, a browser that shrinks the layout viewport, iOS moving the visual viewport, a pinch, turning, rounding, the keyboard closing, the settle and the stop, no visualViewport, the field kept in sight; `sheet.test.ts`: the sheet drawn carries the lift, and nothing without it). design.spec.ts: "a sheet stands over the phone's keyboard (faked)", the phone only, `fakeKeyboard` shrinking `visualViewport.height` and sending its resize, since Playwright opens no keyboard.
- Checked here: `pnpm check`, and the dev server's /design/chat at 375px with the same faked keyboard (the sheet 8px over it and 8px under the top, its field in sight, moved by iOS's offset, nothing for a pinch, back down once closed, the field brought back into the body after the settle, the page behind not scrolled) and at the desktop's width (unchanged). Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright.
- Only a real phone can confirm: iOS Safari and the installed app, Android Chrome; that the lift follows the keyboard without a jump; iOS moving the visual viewport as well; and the chat's composer.
- Defaults applied: the gap over the keyboard 8px, as over the safe area; under a pinch nothing is lifted; the composer left as it is.
- After review: (1) a sheet opened from a button while a field on the page kept the keyboard (the chat's attach button on iOS) flew up to a keyboard about to close, then dropped about 300px during its opening spring. `watchKeyboard` now takes `within` (the sheet's Content, passed to `useKeyboardLift` as the scrim is) and lifts only while a field to type in inside it holds the focus (`opensKeyboard`: an input that is not a button, box, file and the like, a textarea or contenteditable, not read-only), read again on `focusin` and on `focusout` by its `relatedTarget`, so going from one field to the next is no drop; a field that autofocuses (CodeSlots) is inside at once and lifts. (2) Sideways, with some 130px over the keyboard, the header and the `shrink-0` footer left the body no height: the field vanished and the footer went under the keyboard. Below `SHEET_FRAME_MIN` (220px: header, a footer with a large button, one field) `sheetScrollsWhole` makes the Content scroll whole (`overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain`, the body `shrink-0`), and the settle brings the field into sight in the body, then in the sheet (`keepFocusInSheet`). (3) `nearestScroll`'s comment and test now say what it does: a field taller than the view aligns its top when below and its bottom when above (CSSOM). Unit tests for each; checked on the dev server with the faked keyboard: the attach sheet opened from the focused composer is never lifted, the close button or a radio taking the focus drops it, and at 844x360 with 130px left the new message sheet and the choices sheet (its quantity in the footer) scroll whole, the field 12px over the sheet's foot.

## 2026-11-22 · chat · what each business asks of the hiring file, and Today's cards (profile H5b, #82)

- On chat/hiring-file-settings from ba48b60 (H5a's branch chat/hiring-file-me, PR #222, not merged yet; on origin/main 9fccb79 with H4 merged). The design's §1.2, §2.5, §3.1, §3.2, §3.4, §4.3 and §5's H5 row, the H5 plan's §10 and §13 (its default 9: the file's settings are an owner's and the team's), Orel's decisions of 8 November on #1.

- Settings: eleven fields in `modules/employee_files/manifest.ts`, each `level: "owner"`: `askBank` and `askAddress` (on), `askEmergencyContact` and `askWorkLanguage` (off), `form101` (`print`, `link`, `off`), `form101Provider`, `form101Link` (https to a named host, its scheme and host kept in lower case), `payrollProvider`, `employerName`, `employerPhone` (a mobile or a landline, kept as E.164) and `deductionsFile` (nine digits from 9). `homeWidget` is `{ minRole: "worker" }`. `@domain/settings-form` gains the meta `entry` (`link`, `phone`, `digits`): `/manage` and the console draw such a text setting left to right with its keyboard, a phone as people write it, and the activity log says it left to right and "" as "לא נקבע".
- Migration `20261126000400_employee_files_settings.sql`: `private.employee_files_settings` (the manifest's defaults under what the business stored) and `private.file_asks`; `private.file_groups` replaced so `askAddress` and `askBank` decide the groups the file counts (a stored value that isn't a boolean reads as the default), so `file_missing`, the worker's read, the status and the export follow at once; `public.set_module_settings` replaced (20261120000200_order_taking_takers.sql's newest body, one branch after taking orders'): every field an owner's and the team's (42501), no key outside the eleven, each value one the manifest outputs (22023, `private.file_setting_line` and `private.file_setting_link`). Both keep their signatures; revoked and granted again all the same. `database.types.ts` doesn't change (no public signature does). The app works before it reaches a project: everything reads as asked, and a setting saves without the database's checks (the manifest's still apply; the module is off on the hosted project).
- The flow: `@domain/employee-file-settings` (the asks, `fileGroupsAsked`, `form101SetupOf`, the notice's names, `bankChangeToTell`, the seen cookie), `@domain/phone`'s `normalizeIsraeliPhone` and `displayIsraeliPhone`, and the steps' helpers taking the steps on screen (`fileParts`, `fileStepsOn`) with a state `optional`. The address and bank steps go when not asked, unless the file holds them (then said, and they can be changed or emptied); step 2 is "דרכי קשר" without the address, and takes the work language and the emergency contact (saved after the details by `saveEmergencyContact`, only when changed); step 5 by link shows "מילוי הטופס אצל {provider}" (`noopener noreferrer`, the site it opens under it) and "מסרתי את הטופס" marks it with the settings' route, decided on the server; with Form 101 off the step is gone and the done screen sends the file. The notice names the employer, whoever prepares the payroll, the 101's system and the phone. `/me`'s rows follow the parts, with a row for the contact.
- Today: the accent card "התיק שלי ב…" while something is missing (not for owners; flat when the first steps or an open mode hold the accent), else the quiet row says "הפרטים בתיק הושלמו"; a bank-change card for 14 days after `bank_changed_at`, with "לא שיניתי" (`disputeBankChangeAction`, then the card's second state and "הבנתי") and "אני שיניתי", kept per device in `ca_bank_seen` by the database's moment, read again on the server (`seenBankChangeAction`), forgotten on signing out. `currentFileToday` never takes Today down.
- `/join`: after the claim, the business's modules are read with the secret key (`getBusinessModulesForTeam`), and a worker or a manager lands on `/me/file` (the welcome) while the module is on (`landingAfterJoin`); owners and everyone else on `/home`.
- Twins: `/design/shell/me/file?ask=lean|contact&form101=link|off` and `state=ready` (everything in, not sent), `/design/shell/me?file=lean|contact`, `/design/shell/today/worker?file=missing|bank|disputed` with stand-ins that keep nothing, and `/design/shell/manage/employee_files` (the twin's rows now have the module, with an owner's settings).
- Tests: unit for each domain file, the registry, the actions (`actions.test.ts`), the drawn flow in every new state and the notice's names, Today's part and its two cards, the join's landing and the guards; `modules/employee_files/settings.db.test.ts` (`pnpm test:db`: the defaults, the groups by the asks, the database taking exactly what the manifest takes) and pgTAP `employee_files_settings.test.sql` (22); e2e `employee-file.spec.ts` gains four tests (the settings deciding the steps, the owner's 101 link and the worker handing it in there, Today's cards with "לא שיניתי" and per-device hiding, landing on the welcome after `/join`) and design.spec.ts 15 URLs and five walks.
- Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: the migration, pgTAP, `pnpm test:db`, `pnpm build` and Playwright: CI is their first run.
- Changed from the plan: the resolved Form 101 is `Form101Setup` (`form101SetupOf`), since `Form101Route` is already the tax year's route; the link's check is case-sensitive in both places, the manifest lower-casing the scheme and host first, so the two never differ on case folding; a setting's line is trimmed by the manifest rather than refused; the email held keeps step 2 on screen; the twin's `contact` asks the contact and the language instead of the address and the account (so "דרכי קשר" can be seen), and `state=ready` was added for the done screen's send; Today's decision is `todayFilePart`, tested on its own; the bank card's answers can carry the twin's destination (`FileTodayAnswer`); CLAUDE.md is left unchanged (its line on settings could name `entry`).
- Defaults applied (to ask Orel), as recommended: H5 split into H5b and H5c; the 101's link set by owners and the team, any https address until the approved list (H7); the address asked by default everywhere, owners can switch it off; a part no longer asked stays visible to the worker to change or delete; the work language typed, with examples; a link chosen with none set shows the paper card; with Form 101 off the done screen sends; Today's file card for workers and managers until complete, then a quiet row; the bank card 14 days, per device; "לא שיניתי" at once, seen by holders on H6's screens; workers and managers land on the file's welcome after joining; the employer's phone a mobile or a landline.
- After review: `ca_bank_seen` keeps `<business>.<person>.<ms>` and a change is seen only at its exact moment and by its own person (`bankChangeSeen`), so on a shared tablet one worker's "אני שיניתי" never hides another's card, nor an earlier change; `confirmBankAction` reads `bank_changed_at` back once the account is in and keeps it seen on that device (`keepOwnChangeSeen`), so a new worker isn't asked "was it you?" about the account they just typed, while every other device still is. The notice says the managers see the emergency contact while step 2 shows it (`notice.emergency`), and the flow's and `/me`'s note say so too (`visibleEmergency`). A 101 system with no name set is named by its site in `<ltr>` (`provider` null; `form101.openSite`, `linkBodySite`, `notice.form101Site`), and the link's hint no longer has "https://" inside Hebrew (a tooling test holds every Hebrew address outside `<ltr>` to end on a letter or a digit). The done screen's send is said (`done.sent`) and its title takes the focus. The twin's `/manage` lists the modules at the level it draws (the owner's has the file), the activity twins as the owner, with a file's setting line (`textSettingShown` in `@domain/settings-form`, tested). The account no longer asked says so in its step and can be kept or changed; taking it out isn't on screen (ROADMAP 73 says so); `?ask=held` is that twin. The e2e reads the 101's own row (`demoTaxYear`), since a hand-in writes no details row.
- After the second review (7 confirmed findings): "אני שיניתי" and "הבנתי" keep seen only the change the card was drawn for: `FileToday.bank` carries the database's moment (`changedAt`), the card sends it back, and the new route `/home/bank-seen` reads the file again and keeps nothing when the account changed since (409; Today, drawn again, tells the newer change, the card keyed by its moment); the account just confirmed is kept seen by the moment its own save was dated with (`saveEmployeeDetails` `atTime`, which `set_employee_details` keeps as `bank_changed_at`), never one read afterwards. The card hides as the first steps hide: at once, "הכרטיס הוסתר במכשיר הזה" with "ביטול" focused, the write held (`useHeldWrite`) and sent through the route, since a held action loses its Set-Cookie (`sendHide` now shared, `home/hide-request.ts`); "ביטול" brings it back with the focus on the button pressed; `seenBankChangeAction` is gone. The bank step on /me/file offers "לא שיניתי" under the saved account while the change is told, on every device (`FileScreenData.bankChange`, `BankChangeNote`), so hiding the card never takes the way to dispute. Who may write the file is shared by the actions and the route (`me/file/file-writer.ts`). A fund chosen "later" all that is left (`onlyPensionLeft`, `pensionLeftOf`): Today's card and the profile's say "נשאר לבחור קרן פנסיה עד …" with "בחירת קרן פנסיה" to `?step=pension`, and the done screen, once sent, is titled "נשאר לבחור קרן פנסיה" with that as its main action (before sending, the send stays first, since with Form 101 off the done screen is the only place the file goes, and it says the fund can come later: `done.readyPension`). The progress line counts an optional step only once it is done ("0 מתוך 4"), as the welcome and the bar count the steps. The file card's button is secondary when the card is flat, so the screen keeps one accent action. Form 101 by link: "מסרתי את הטופס" full width on a phone, as the link's button. Twins: Today `?file=pension` and `?file=complete`, the file `?state=later` (and `/me?file=later`), the file twin's way back by what the business asks, and Today's bank twin hides with its undo through a stand-in route that keeps nothing. Unit tests (the route, the actions' own moment, `stepsDone`, `onlyPensionLeft`, the cards, the done screen, the bank step, the progress, the 101 widths); e2e (the hold and its undo, a change made while the card was open told again, the cookie, "לא שיניתי" in the bank step, the second device) and design.spec.ts (four new URLs, three new walks, two updated).
- After the final check (two confirmed): a fund chosen "later" whose day passed is said everywhere, since the database still counts it missing and Today still asks: `pensionLaterOf` (the answer "later" and the pension missing, by `pensionDue`) drives the flow's pension row ("לבחור קרן פנסיה. המועד לבחירה עבר", `done.leftPensionPassed`), a desktop's summary ("פנסיה" listed, never "לא חסר כלום", with the day's line) and the done screen, titled "נשאר לבחור קרן פנסיה" by its day or with it passed, never "סיימת" or "הכול מוכן" (`pensionDueWords`, shared with the step); Today's card and `/me`'s take the same words (`completeWords`), the passed one "המועד לבחור קרן פנסיה עבר, ועדיין אפשר לבחור" (`me.file.pensionPassed`), and the step's `pension.duePassed` now says a fund can still be chosen. "לא שיניתי" is never asked under the person's own change: the bank screen keeps `changedHere` apart from the account shown (`bankMoves`: confirmed sets it, "החלפת החשבון" and "השארת החשבון הנוכחי" leave it), and on a reload the seen cookie tells a change made on this device from one only said seen on Today (`ca_bank_seen` entries end `.here` for an account added here: `bankChangeSeenAs`, `FileScreenData.bankChange.seen`, read once in `currentFileScreen`, which Today now uses too), so the step still asks after "אני שיניתי", as the second review wanted, and not after the person's own account. Tests: unit (the done screen, the row and the summary open and passed, sent and not, the cards' shared words, `pensionLaterOf`, the cookie's two kinds, confirm → "החלפת החשבון" → "השארת החשבון הנוכחי" drawn with the change told). A reason under every field, "לא חובה" beside the optional ones, the notice "למה מבקשים את הפרטים האלה" one tap away on every step, the saved ID and account as their last digits with "הצגה" for a minute. Phone: one step under the steps bar, its main action floating above the tab bar; tablet: the steps beside the one on screen; desktop: every step and a sticky summary with the page's one main action.

## 2026-11-21 · chat · taking orders: offline (waiter mode P7)

- On chat/order-offline from origin/main e5a0d06 (P6's branch, PR #213, on top of main e988dd8 with P1 to P5 merged). Orel's decisions of 20 November: every phase, §16's recommendations. No migration: `database.types.ts` unchanged, no pgTAP file, no Migrate run.
- The outbox (`app/(app)/orders/outbox.ts`): one per page, outside React, the only writer of the drafts, the rounds on their way, their refusals and the menu kept on the phone. The mode's provider reads it through `useSyncExternalStore`, so a mode hidden in an `<Activity>` or drawn afresh reads the same, and P4's `claim`/`keep` is gone. `OrdersOutbox` in the `(app)` layout starts it for whoever takes orders, so a killed app sends what waited as it opens, on any screen. A round goes at once and again at 1, 2, 5, 10 and 30 s, woken by `online` and the page shown, with one last keepalive as it hides (64 KB in all until shown again, once each); it says "עוד לא נשלח" only once its strict write is done, and the screen's send lets go then, so another may follow offline. After 10 minutes it checks one's own rounds (`order_mine`, whose ids are the keys) and asks: "שליחה" (again after another 10) or "הסרה מהטלפון" with "ביטול". A refusal goes back to its draft's front with its reason in one transaction with the settle, once across tabs. 401 and `notMember` take the person's rounds and menu off the phone (all businesses, or that one), drafts kept, with a one-time notice of place names in sessionStorage (`orders-lost`) on the app's banner, the door and the no-business screen; 409 `person` holds them in the page and draws the frame again.
- The store (`orders-db.ts`): IndexedDB `conceptapp-orders` v1, `rounds`, `drafts` (with the refusal and whether its screen showed it) and `menus`, each record with its person and business, read back through `@domain/orders-outbox`'s schemas; other tabs hear through `BroadcastChannel`; `memoryOrdersDb` where IndexedDB is missing or blocked, and in the tests. Another person's rounds, and any draft or menu, go after 16 hours untouched; one's own never silently.
- The server: `sendBody` gains a required `user`; `/orders-send` refuses another than the session's (409 `person`) before `viewOnly`, and a business not among `getOwnPlace`'s new `memberOf` (403 `notMember`), both before the database.
- Session ends (`orders-storage.ts`, which imports only the store): signing out asks once when an order of the person's hasn't gone ("יציאה ומחיקה" / "חזרה"), then clears their records in every business and what expired, awaited before the page goes; locking the clock station clears everyone's, after a warning line with how many and where. Both remove P4's `orders:` sessionStorage entries, which the outbox's first open also drops.
- Screens: a round on its way is "בשליחה", "עוד לא נשלח" or the question (`RoundState`, also over the items on a phone); the phone opens on the order when a round is on its way; the floor's tile "לא נשלח" (`PlaceTile` tone `unsent`), first on the status line, in the desktop table and the order's list of places, and the new takeaway's tile while its first round waits; a count on "שולחנות"; "אין חיבור" (`ConnectionNote`, §7.4's words) under the title of the floor, the order, "ההזמנות שלי", the prep screen and the till; outside the mode a banner above the screen (`OutboxBannerView`, never a toast) with "פתיחה", and each round that lands said to screen readers. The menu is kept on the phone and seeds the mode at once.
- The sync's pace (`ordersSyncPace`): 5 s while a round of one's own of an open order has a ticket at its department with lines left (doubling to 60), and on the prep screen (doubling to 30); else 15.
- Pure: `@domain/orders-outbox` (`rowTurn`, `askAge`, `sendVerdict`, `keepsRecord`, `sentAlready`, the schemas), `ordersSyncPace`, `nextOrdersSyncDelay`'s `pace`. The twin: states `asking`, `offline` and `lost`, its sender's `answer`/`remove`/`unsent`, five index rows; the gallery's "לא נשלח" tile.
- Tests: unit for the domain, the sync's pace and body, the route (`user` required, `person`, `notMember`), `getOwnPlace`'s `memberOf`, the store in memory, the engine (`outbox.test.ts`: kept before waiting, offline then online once, the retry waits, a refusal back and kept, another tab's settle, the question, `answer`, `remove`/`restore`, a round that went already, another person, a session switch, `person`, viewing, lost by 401 and by `notMember`, the hide's keepalive and its budget, expiry, another tab's message, a change before the first read, the menu, no store), the session ends, the send store's new fields, the twin, and a toast case; e2e `orders-offline.spec.ts` (five flows) with the shared helpers moved to `e2e/orders-parts.ts`, `orders.spec.ts`'s direct sends with `user` and the new refusals, `e2e/db.ts` `demoRoundSenders`, design.spec five URLs and a walk. Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright: first in CI.
- Changed from the plan: the refusal kept on a draft also keeps where its round went, so the banner names its place; keepalive's 64 KB are shared until the page shows again, not per hide; a round a screen waits for settles by its own answer rather than by the sync's `mine` (one toast); the sign-out sheet's title is `outbox.unsent` with the places under it, and its close button is "חזרה"; the station lock reads what hasn't gone as its sheet opens.
- For Orel, not applied (CLAUDE.md): `PlaceTile`'s `unsent` tone, `OutboxBannerView`/`LostOrdersNoticeView` and `ConnectionNote` for "Built so far".
- Defaults applied (to ask Orel): drafts kept on the phone per person and business until sent or 16 hours; sign-out asks once and says what goes; another person's waiting order waits for them, up to 16 hours, shown to nobody else; "הסרה מהטלפון" removes with "ביטול" for 8 seconds; after "שליחה" another 10 minutes asks again; every 5 seconds while a round of one's own is in preparation and on the prep screen; no other table offline in P7; a banner outside the mode, no toast; a 401 removes the person's waiting orders with a notice; another business's order waits for that business; locking the station removes everything after saying how many; "אין חיבור" as a card under the title. The lost notice shows where the refresh keeps the app's frame too: `keepLostNotice` says so on the window and `LostOrdersNotice` reads through `useSyncExternalStore` (`lost-notice.ts`); it has its own name (`outbox.lost.label`, two asides were one landmark), and is mounted after the outbox, whose live region comes first and is `aria-live` without `role="status"` (schedule-build.spec's status locator), so a card stays right above `main` and its glow. The sign-out sheet keeps its words while it closes. Sign-out asks about and clears the records of the session's person as the server says, never the page's outbox person, which a page with no outbox (another business, the console, the no-business screen after a reload) didn't know and the cookie could leave stale: `signOut("who")` names the person, asked only when the phone holds a round that hasn't gone (`unsentToAsk`), and `signOut()` answers `{ to, user }`, the session it ended (`sessionUserHere`, read first); `ordersPerson` is gone, and the design's stand-ins name nobody (no outbox starts on `/design`). e2e: the person changes with the cookies cleared (the door sends a signed-in browser home, and the last send as the page leaves finds no session); the ten-minute flow asks before its reload (the page told its rows changed, as another tab would) and keeps its sends aborted until "שליחה"; the sign-out flow reaches `/me` by the shell's link, so no last send leaves as a page hides. The Rounds comment's cancelled line is back. - P7's demo (§14) in the product: "switching demo person keeps it unsent" holds where a session ends without signing out here (expired, or the cookies cleared, as the e2e does), since the door opens only signed out and signing out asks and takes the person's orders off the phone. - After the re-review of those fixes (each finding confirmed):
  - A new takeaway's first round: its send waits while that round is on its way, whichever send it was (`sendWaits`), so a screen shown again, mounted afresh or reloaded opens no second takeaway; a round that went again with no screen waiting says "נשלחה" with "פתיחה" once it lands (never over a way back), and the screen that showed it on its way goes to its order (`sender.landed`); a send answered after its screen was left gets "פתיחה" too.
  - The tab's entry has one writer, the mode that read it last (`claim`/`keep` in orders-storage.ts): one Next dropped from its cache settles a late answer in its own memory only.
  - A ticket put back here stays out of the ready news until a sync started after the put-back answered has come (`readyMarks`, ready-marks.ts), so a slower sync from before it says nothing.
  - The mode's toast is a pure controller now (`modeToast`, mode-toast.ts): a withdrawal takes away its own toast alone and tells the gate at once, an older toast seen going after a newer one was said lets no news out, and the tests hold it (a newer serve's "ביטול" kept, the withdrawn take-back's write unheard); the news-gate test that only repeated the gate's path is gone.
- After the re-review of a898aab (each finding confirmed):
  - A send answered after its screen was left says "נשלחה" through the news gate (`tell` on the mode's value), so it never takes the place of a take-back's or a serve's "ביטול" on the screen now shown.
  - A withdrawn toast lets the news out only once what withdrew it is done (a microtask): the next take-back's toast, said in the same turn, keeps the ready news waiting instead of hiding it.
  - The gate keeps what waits by what it is about ("ready", `sent:<round>`) and tells each in turn, the next once the toast before it has gone, so a round that landed and the ready news no longer drop each other.
  - A ticket marked ready here is news again once a read made after the mark sees it not ready (`readyMarks.seen`): put back and made ready again on another phone, its owner is told.
- After the re-review of 8731add (each finding confirmed): a serve's "ביטול" is no ready news (`readyNews` leaves out a ticket served before and ready again with the time it was ready at; one served new and marked ready since still is); a toast whose time is up is forgotten (`modeToast.hidden` puts null), and a `StatusToast` drawn again with the same message (its screen shown again from a hidden `<Activity>`) goes on with the time it had left and never takes the focus again, so no finished "מוכן" or stale "ביטול" comes back on returning to the mode; the send's "נשלחה" on its own screen goes through the news gate too, so a take-back's or a serve's "ביטול" said while the round was on its way stays until it goes.
